- 1 -

I
ON THE LOGICAL USE OF THE SINTHOME OR
FREUD WITH JOYCE
论圣状的逻辑作用或弗洛伊德与乔伊斯的关系
Joyce, that father-tasked wretch
The body: form and the fact of saying
The father is a sinthome
On the four-component Borromean link
On an art that foils the truth of the sinthome

乔伊斯，那个被父亲责备的可怜虫
身体：形式和言说的事实
父亲是一个圣状
论四组分的波罗米结
论揭示出圣状真相的艺术

Sinthome is an old way of spelling what was subsequently
spelt Symtâme.
This way of spelling it marks a date that of the injection of
Greek into what I call my lalingua, specifically French. Indeed,
I've allowed myself to alter its spelling in this way because



- 2 -

Joyce, in the first chapter of Ulysses, expressed his wish to
Hellenize, to inject in like fashion the Hellenic tongue, but into
what? We don't know, since it wasn't Gaelic, even though
Ireland was involved.

Sinthome（圣状）是法语中 Symtâme（症状）的一种古
老的拼写方式。
这一拼写方式标志了一个时期，即希腊语被吸收进我
所谓的牙牙语（lalangue），尤其是法语。 事实上，我之所
以改变它的拼写，是因为乔伊斯在《尤利西斯》的第一章
表达了他希望（语言）被希腊化，以类似的方式吸收希腊
语，但注入什么语言呢？我们不知道，因为乔伊斯没有用
爱尔兰盖尔语（Gaelic）写作，尽管他是爱尔兰人。

Joyce had to write in English, there's no doubt about that.
However , as someone whom I do hope is here in the audience
Philippe Sollers, has remarked in Tel Que/, Joyce wrote in
English in such a way that the English language no longer
exist..,.

他必须用英语写作。然而，正如菲利普·索尔莱斯
（Philippe Sollers），应该就坐在下边，他在《Tel Quel》杂



- 3 -

志中说，乔伊斯用英语写作的方式让英语不复存在了。

Admittedly, this tongue was already one that bore little
consist­ ency, which doesn't mean that it's easy to write in
English, but through the sequence of works that he penned in
English, Joyce added something to it that makes the same author
say that it ought to be written l'élangues, I suppose he thereby
means to designate something like the elation that we are told
lies at the root of a sort of sinthome that in psychiatry we call
mania.

可以肯定，（他所使用的）这种语言的一致性几乎消失
了。这并不是说用英语写作很容易，而是乔伊斯通过用英
语书写的一系列作品，为英语增加了一些东西。索莱尔斯
说这应该被写作方言（l’élangues）。我想他是在指称类似狂
喜的状态，在精神病学中这被称做狂躁（mania），他欺骗我
们说狂躁是一种圣状的根源。

Mania is indeed what Joyce's last work looks like, the one
that he strung out for such a long while to attract the attention of
all and sundry, namely Finnegans Wake.




- 4 -

乔伊斯最后一部作品《芬尼根守灵夜》看起来确实很
狂躁，这是他魔怔了这么长时间所创作的令众人瞩目的作
品。

It was in relation to this work that I let myself get roped
into opening a Joyce symposium, given what I must say was a
pressing request from Jacques Aubert, who is present here today
and every bit as pressing. This was also how, all told, I let
myself be turned away from the project I announced at the end
of the last academic year, which was to call this year's Seminar 4,
5, 6. I've made do with four, and I'm glad because I would surely
have been defeated by 4, 5, 6.This doesn't mean that the four at
issue shall sit any more lightly on my shoulders.

与这部作品有关的是，雅克·奥伯特(Jacques Aubert)，
他今天也在场，因为他迫切的请求，我不得不强迫自己参
加了一个乔伊斯研讨会的开幕式。这也（就解释了）我是
如何从我上一学年末宣布的项目，即今年的研讨班4、5、6，
中抽身出来的。我完成了研讨班4，对此我很庆幸，因为4、
5、6 这三个研讨班对我来说难以驾驭。不过这并不是说研
讨班4 相对轻松。




- 5 -

1

I have inherited from Freud, quite in spite of myself, for
having stated in my time what could be gleaned with sound
logic from the gibberish of those he called his band, and whom
I've no need to name. This was the clique that attended the
Vienna meetings, and it can't be said that a single one of them
followed the path of what I'm calling sound logic.

继承自弗洛伊德——尽管我自己也是如此，但我说过，
在我的时代，可以从那些不值一提的所谓弗洛伊德派学者
的胡言乱语中以合乎逻辑的方式收集到些什么。这些就是
参加维也纳会议的那伙人，可以说，他们中没有一个人遵
循了我所说的合乎逻辑的方式。

To cut to the chase, I shall say that what specifies Nature
per se is that it is not a nature, hence logical process as a means
of broaching it. Through the process of calling Nature that
which you exclude by the very fact of taking an interest in
something, this something becoming differentiated on account
of being named, Nature ventures nothing save to affirm itself as
a potpourri of what is not in the nature of anything.



- 6 -


直截了当地说，“自然（nature）”本身不是一种自然，
因此逻辑过程是探讨它的一种手段。通过把某物称为“自然”，
你们排除了对某物感兴趣的这一事实，这个某物由于被命
名而有了差异，自然除了是不属于任何性质的一锅粥外，
什么也不是。

This statement has one advantage, which is the following.
Should you find, fairly reckoning, that naming stands apart from
what seems to be the law of Nature, that man doesn't enjoy any
relation that is naturally sexual - this naturally comes with every
reservation - well, this statement allows you logically to posit
that this is not exclusive to man, which does actually turn out to
be the case.

这种说法的好处如下：你应该发现了，公平地估计，
“命名”（naming）似乎与自然法则相悖，人类不存在任何自
然的性关系——这天然地被保留了下来——那么，这一论
断在逻辑上假设这不是人所独有的，这确实造就了一种独
有的情况。

Take care, however, not to start saying that there is nothing



- 7 -

natural about sex. Try instead to find out how it is in each case,
from bacteria to birds, because they do have names. I've already
alluded to both one and the other.

然而，请注意，不要说性和自然没关系。相反，要努
力在细菌到鸟类中的每一种情况，因为它们确实拥有名字。
我已经暗指了这两种情况。

Let's note in passing that in the Creation that is said to be
Divine only in that it makes reference to naming, the bacterium
is not named. Nor is it named when, fooling around with the
putative original man, God suggests he begin by the act of
saying the name of each little beastie.

让我们顺便注意一下，被称为神圣的造物只是因为它
被命名了，而细菌并没有被命名。在与假设存在的原始人
开玩笑时，上帝建议从每个小动物的名字开始，但却没有
给细菌命名。

The only trace we have of this first bout of mucking around
lies in concluding from it that Adam, as his name indicates well
enough — an allusion to Peirce's function of the index - was a



- 8 -

Madam, precisely in keeping with the joke that Joyce makes.

关于第一个混淆，我们唯一的线索就是由此得出的结
论：亚当（ADAM）——正如他的名字所示——参考自皮
尔斯索引的典故——是一位女士（Madam），这恰恰与乔伊
斯开的玩笑一致。

Indeed, one does have to assume that Adam, only ever
named the... beasts in the tongue of she that I shall call l’Évie.
I've every right to call her this because in Hebrew, if Hebrew is
indeed a tongue, her name means mother of the living. Well,
l’Évie found her tongue straightaway, and kept it well-oiled,
because after the putative naming by Adam she was the first
person to put it to use, in order to speak to the serpent.

事实上，我们不得不假设，亚当只用她的语言为野兽
命名，我将称之为埃维（l’Évie）1。我完全有资格这样称呼
她，因为在希伯来语中，她名字的意义是生灵之母（mother
of the living）的意思。好吧，埃维直接找到了她的语言，并
顺利地运作着，因为在被亚当命名之后，她第一个使用这
种语言的人——用来与蛇说话。

1 l’Evie 与les vies（lives 生命，生活）发音相近。



- 9 -


This so-called Divine Creation is thus redoubled by the
parley of the parlêtre, as I've called him, by which l’Évie turns
the serpent into what you'll allow me to call the serre-fesses, the
buttock-clencher, later designated as a faille, a fault-line, or
better still, a phallus - because you need one to make the faut-
pas.

因此，这种所谓的神圣造物通过我所称之为的言说的
存在（parlêtre）而加倍，通过它，埃维将蛇变成了你允许
我称之为serre-fesses，即臀部夹紧的东西，后来被称为
faille（断裂）, fault-line（断层线），或者最好的还是——
phallus（菲勒斯），因为你需要一个东西来做不该做的事
（faut-pas）。

This is the fault, the sin, which my sinthome
advantageously starts with. In English, sin refers to the trespass
of original sin, hence the necessity of the fact that the fault-line
that is always growing doesn't stop, unless it should undergo the
stop of castration as possible.

这是我的错误，即罪，也就是我的圣状有机可乘的开



- 10 -

始。在英语中，罪指的是原罪的过失，因此必须有不断增
长的断层线（fault-line）它永无止息，除非它能尽可能地忍
受阉割的停止。

I said in the past that this possible is what stops being
written. Seeing you here in such large numbers I reckon there
must all the same be some who have already heard my yarns,
but you haven't remotely noticed, since I myself didn't, that a
comma has to be included here. The possible is what stops,
comma, being written. Or rather, what would stop, taking the
path of being written in the event that the discourse I have
mentioned would at last come to be, a discourse such that it is
not semblance.

我过去说过，这可能是停止被写出的东西（this
possible is what stops being written）2。看到来了这么多人，
我估计肯定也有一些人已经听过我的唠叨，但你们还没有
注意到，因为连我自己也没有，这里必须加上一个逗号。
“可能是什么阻止了，逗号，被写出（The possible is what
stops, comma, being written.）。”或者说，是什么阻止了，走
上了被书写的道路，它在我所提到的话语中走向了存在，

2 见第二十期研讨班Encore 第八章。（译注）



- 11 -

并且这一话语不仅仅是外在的。

Is it impossible for truth ever to be produced by savoir-faire?
No, it isn't, but then it shall only be half-said, incarnated in a
signifier S subscript 1, right where it takes at least two of them
for The Woman, the one and only, She who is mythical in the
sense that the myth makes her singular - it's Eve, whom I spoke
about just now - ever to have been incontestably possessed, for
having tasted the fruit of the forbidden tree, the tree of
Knowledge.

真理永远不可能经由做的知识（savoir-faire）3得以产生
吗？不，它可以，但它只能被半说，体现在能指S1 中，至
少需要它们中的两者，以便那个独一无二的一方，也就是
女人（La femme）4，女人（La femme）在某种意义上总是
已然成为神话，即神话使她成为（独特的）个体（singular）
——就是我刚才谈到的夏娃——总是无可争议地被占有，
因为尝到了禁忌之树——知识之树的果实。


3 savoir-faire 的原意是本事，才能，才干，本领（译注）
4 拉康在这里断言，大写的"Woman"（即La femme，普遍的女性（性）），即本
质上是单数的"Woman"，并不存在（普遍的女性不存在）；"Woman"（La femme）
作为一个包罗万象的概念（一种柏拉图式的形式）是一种幻觉。有多种多样的
女性，但没有"女性"或"女人味"的本质（见Encore 第六章）（译注）



- 12 -

L’Évie, then, is not mortal. She is no more mortal than
Socrates. The Woman in question is another name of God, and it
is in this respect that she doesn't exist, as I've said many times.

那么，埃维不是凡人。她不比苏格拉底更像凡人。女
人（La femme）是上帝的另一个名字，在这个意义上，她
不存在，我已经说过很多次了。

You will notice Aristotle's cunning here, not wanting the
singular to play a role in his logic. Now, contrary to what he
allowed in the aforesaid logic, it has to be said that Socrates is
not a man, because he agrees to die so that the city will live on.
He accepts this, and this is a fact. Moreover, it really has to be
said that on this occasion he doesn't want to hear his wife speak.
Hence my formula about woman, which I'm pegging out, as it
were, for your use, by using the μὴ πáντες, which is the
opposition, dismissed by Aristotle, to the universal of the πáν,
and which I picked out from the Organon.

你会注意到亚里士多德在这里的狡猾，他不想让这一
个体在他的逻辑中扮演一个角色。但是与他上述逻辑中所
允许的相反，必须说苏格拉底不是一个人，因为他同意赴



- 13 -

死，以使得城邦能够继续存在，因为他接受了这一点，这
是事实。此外还必须提到，在那个场合他不想听他妻子说
话。因此，我所挑出的关于女人的公式，恰如为所你们所
用的，通过对μὴ πάντες [mí pántes]的使用，它被亚里士多
德所弃用，并且与πάν [pán]的普遍性相对立，而我从《工
具论》中将其挑选了出来。

I haven't managed to find it again, but I did read it there,
sure enough that my daughter, who is here today, put her finger
on it and swore to me earlier that she would find the place again
for me. Woman is not all but in the equivocal form that takes on
its piquant from this lalangue of ours, in the form of but not that,
as when one says, anything, but not that. This was precisely
Socrates' position. The but not that is what, under this year's title,
I'm intro­ ducing as the sinthome.

我没有再设法找它，但我确实在书里读到过，当然，
我的女儿，她今天在这里，早些时候她把手放在书上向我
发誓说会为我再次找到它在哪。“女人”不是整全的（La
femme n’est toute），而是以模棱两可的形式，从我们的牙牙
语（lalangue）中获得了她的魅力（piquant），但并非以那个
形式，就像人们说：“可以是任何东西，但不是它。”（tout,



- 14 -

mais pas ça !）这正是苏格拉底的立场。在今年的标题下，
我把这种“但不是它（mais pas ça !）”称为“圣状”。

At this instant, at the instance of the letter such as it has so
far been outlined- and don't expect any better, because whatever
might be more effective would do no better than to displace the
sinthome, even to multiply it - for the present instance, there is
the sinthome madaquin, which I spell however you like.

在这个时候，在迄今为止已经概述过的字符的例子中--
不要指望有什么更好的，因为任何可能东西都不会比
sinthome 这个词有更好的效果，甚至只会使这个词更有效—
—在目前的例子中，有一个sinthome madaquin，我按照你
们想要的方式拼写它5。

You know that Joyce had slaved quite enough over this
saint homme. As far as philosophy is concerned, no one's ever
done any better. One has to put things as best one can - it's the
only true thing there is. This doesn't prevent Joyce from not
really making head or tail of it with respect to the thing by

5 这里是指托马斯·阿奎那（Thomas D’Aquin）拉康拿他的名字玩的一个拼写
游戏



- 15 -

which he sets such great store, namely what he calls Beauty. On
this point, have a look at Jacques Aubert's book and you'll see
that in the sinthome madaquinisme there is goodness knows
what exactly which he calls claritas, which Joyce replaces with
something along the lines of the radiance of Being, which is
really the weak point at issue.

你知道，乔伊斯在这个圣人（saint homme）身上耕耘
得够多了。就哲学而言，从来没有人（比他）做得更好。
人们必须尽可能地把事情说清楚——这是唯一真实的事情。
但这并不妨碍乔伊斯对他所看重的东西，即他所说的 "美"，
无法真正地做到有始有终。在这一点上，看看雅克·奥伯特
的书，你会发现在sinthome madaquin 中，天知道他所说的
claritas 到底是什么，乔伊斯用沿着存在的“光芒”之物来代
替，这确实是这一问题的薄弱点。

Is this a personal weakness? I don't find the radiance of
Being to be all that striking. And it is in precisely this respect
that Joyce strips the sinthome of its masaquinism.

这是个人的弱点吗？我并不觉得存在的光芒有多么醒
目。而恰恰是在这方面，乔伊斯剥去了“圣状”的面具



- 16 -

（masaquinism）。

Contrary to how things might appear at first glance, namely
somewhat detached from politics, this produced what I shall call
the sint'home rule. The Freeman's Journal represented this Home
Rule as a sun rising behind the Bank of Ireland. As if by chance,
Joyce has it come up in the northwest, which is not usual for a
sunrise. Despite the squeaking that we meet on this subject in
Joyce, it's none the less the sinthome roule, the rolling sinthome,
on rollers, that Joyce conjoins.

与第一眼看到的情况相反，即有点脱离政治，这产生
了我所说的圣·地方自治（sint-home rule）。《自由人杂志》
（The Freeman's Journal）将这种地方自治表现为爱尔兰银
行背后升起的太阳。仿佛是偶然的，乔伊斯让它在西北方
向升起，这对日出来说是不正常的。尽管我们在乔伊斯的
这个主题上遇到了啸叫，但乔伊斯连接的依然是sinthome
roule，即在滚轮上滚动的圣—家（sint-home）。

It's quite certain that these two terms could be given
different names. I'm naming them this way on account of the
two aspects available to Joyce's art, which will be keeping us



- 17 -

busy this year in view of what I said earlier, which I introduced,
and which I did no better than call by the name he deserves, that
suits him, by changing its spelling.

很肯定的是，这两个术语可以被赋予不同的名称。我
这样命名是考虑到乔伊斯的艺术有两个方面可以利用，鉴
于我前面所说的，今年会让我们忙得不可开交，我介绍了
这一点，我没有更好的办法，只是通过改变它的拼写，用
恰如其分的、适合他的名字称呼他。

Both spellings concern him, but it's a fact that Joyce makes
a choice, and in this regard he is, like me, a heretic. For haeresis
is precisely what specifies the heretic. One has to choose the
path by which to capture the truth - especially since, once the
choice has been made, no one is prevented from subjecting it to
confirmation, that is, from being a heretic in the right way.

两种拼法都与他有关，但乔伊斯做出了选择，这是事
实，在这方面，他和我一样，是个异端。因为haeresis 恰恰
是指明了异端的东西。一个人必须选择抓住真理的道路--尤
其是一旦做出选择，没有人可以阻止它受到确认，也就是
说，没有人可以以正确的方式成为异端。



- 18 -


The right way is the one that, when the nature of the
sinthome has been recognized, doesn't shrink from using it
logically, that is, from using it to the point of reaching its real, at
the end of which it is sated.

正确的方法是，认识到圣状的天性（nature）后，从逻
辑上使用它，不缩手缩脚，也就是说，通过使用它到达到
它的实在，在它的尽头被满足。

2

Joyce did that, but in an approximate fashion, of course,
because one couldn't get off to a worse start than he did.

乔伊斯做到了这一点，当然是以一种渐进的方式，因
为人们无法从一个比他更糟的起点出发：

Being born in Dublin with a boozing father who was more
or less a Fenian, that is, a fanatic, from two families, because
that's how it turns out for everyone when one is a son of two
families and when it so happens that one believes oneself to be



- 19 -

male because one has a little scrap of a dick. Pardon me for this
word, but naturally it takes more than that. However, since his
dick was a bit lax, as it were, his art supplemented his phallic
allure. And this is always the way. The phallus is the conjunction
between what I've called this parasite, which is the little scrap of
a dick in question, and the function of speech. And it is in this
respect that his art is the true guarantor of his phallus.

出生在都柏林、父亲酗酒、或多或少是芬尼亚人
（Fenian）6——也就是狂热分子、来自两个家庭——因为
当一个人是两个家庭的儿子，而且由于他有一个细小的阴
茎（请原谅我用这个词，但它能更好地表达意思）这恰好
使他认为自己是男性，对每个人来说都会有这样的结果。
然而，由于他的阴茎有些痿，而他的艺术补充了他的阳刚
之气。而这始终是一种可行的方式。菲勒斯（phallus）是我
所说的这种寄生虫，也就是成问题的一小段细小的阴茎，
与语言功能的结合。正是在这一点上，他的艺术是菲勒斯
的真正保障者。

Aside from that, let's say that he was a pauvre hère, a poor
wretch, and even a pauvre hérétique. The Joyceans who have

6 一种在北爱尔兰的对天主教徒的蔑称



- 20 -

enjoyment of his heresy are only to be found in academia. But
Joyce quite deliberately wanted this breed to busy themselves
with him. The best of it is that he managed, and beyond all
measure. It has lasted, and will last further still. He expressly
wanted three hundred years of it. He said he wanted to keep the
critics busy for three hundred years, and this he shall achieve,
provided God doesn't atomise us.

此外，让我们说他是一个贫民窟的人（pauvre hère），
一个可怜虫。甚至是一个可怜的异教徒（pauvre hérétique.）。
享受他异端邪说的乔伊斯的追随者只存在于学术界。但乔
伊斯故意让这批人为他忙活。重要的是，他成功了，而且
极其成功。这已经持续了（颇有一段时间）并将进一步延
续下去。他明确地希望是三百年的时间，想让批评家们忙
活三百年，只要上帝不惩罚（atomise）我们。

Ce hère - one cannot say cet hère, the aspirated h for bids it
and this even bothers everyone so much that they say le pauvre
hère - this wretch conceived of himself as a hère-o. Stephen
Hero is the title expressly given to the book from which he
prepared A Portrait of the Artist as a Young Man.




- 21 -

Ce hère（穷人），人们说不出Ce hère 这个短语，这个
送气的h 妨碍了发音，并且这让每个人如此烦恼，以至于他
们要说成：这个可怜虫（le pauvre hère）——这个可怜虫会
把自己想象成一个英— 雄（hère-o ）。斯蒂芬· 英雄
（Stephen Hero）是他为《一位青年艺术家的肖像》这本书
特意起的标题。

I would have liked at least to show you the edition to get
but I didn't bring the book along, which is really daft of me. It's
hard to find, which is why I'm telling you how much you will
have to insist. Nicole Sels, who is here today, sent me a very
precise note - more what you'd call a letter - where over two
pages she explains that currently it's impossible to get hold of
this text, along with its criticism by different people, all of
whom are academics. Besides, to write on Joyce is one way of
getting into the university. The university draws in Joyceans. It
gives them degrees. They're already in a good position. In short,
you won't find the ... - I don't know how to pronounce this name.
Jacques Aubert will tell me. Is it Beeb or Beeb-ie?

我本想至少给你们看看要买的版本，但我没有把书带
来，我真实太笨了。它很难找到，这就是为什么我告诉你，



- 22 -

不管花多少钱你们都要坚持把它买下来。今天在这里的
Nicole Sels 给我发了一个非常精确的说明——更像是你们所
说的信——她在两页纸上解释说，目前不可能拿到这个文
本，以及不同学者对它的批评。此外，写乔伊斯相关的文
章是进入大学的一种方式。大学吸引了乔伊斯的研究者。
它授予他们学位。他们已经获得了一个很好的饭碗。总之，
你不会找到......我不知道这个名字怎么发音。雅克·奥伯特会
告诉我。是Beeb 还是Beeb-ie？

- Usually, one says Beeb-ie.

—通常情况下，人们说Beeb-ie。

He's at the top of the list, with an article on Joyce that's in a
class of its own. Further down you have Hugh Kenner who in
my opinion speaks fairly well about Joyce, perhaps because of
the Saint Thomas Aquinas in question. And then there are others
right up to the end. I regret that you won't be able to have this
book at your disposal.

他位居榜首，有一篇关于乔伊斯的文章，堪称一绝。
再往下是休·肯纳，在我看来，他对乔伊斯的评价相当好，



- 23 -

也许是因为有关圣托马斯·阿奎那的原因。然后一直到书的
最后还有其他人。遗憾的是，你们无法拥有这本书来进行
阅读。

Truth be told, it was a schoolboy blunder on my part, if
ever there was one, to have shrunk the characters of this short
note. You'll have to make arrangements with Nicole Sels to get a
series of photocopies made. As I reckon there are not that many
of you who are up to scratch in English, especially Joyce's
English, this should only be a small number, but clearly there
will be some competition, my goodness, legitimate competition.

说实话，这是我一个学生的错误，如果有的话，我用
小的字体写了些笔记。你们必须与Nicole Sels 商量好，以
获得一系列的照片复印件。由于我估计你们中没有多少人
的英语水平达到要求，特别是乔伊斯使用的英语，符合要
求的人应该只是少部分，但显然还会有一些竞争，我的天，
合法的竞争。

A Portrait of the Artist . . . You have to write Artist with all
the stress on the. This the is not entirely the same as our French
definite article of course. But one can trust Joyce. If he says the,



- 24 -

it's because he really reckons that, when it comes to the artist,
he's the only one. In this, he is singular.

艺术家的肖像 ......你必须把 "艺术家 "写在所有的重音
上。当然，这个 "the "与我们的法语定语不完全相同。但人
们可以相信乔伊斯。如果他说 "the"，那是因为他真的认为，
说到艺术家，他是唯一的一个。在这一点上，他是独一无
二的。

... As a Young Man is very suspect. In French, that would
translate as comme. In other words, what is at issue is the
comme-ment, the how.

......作为一个年轻人（《As》 a Young Man）是非常可疑
的。在法语中，As 被译为comme。换句话说，尚待争议的
是comme-ment，也就是如何（how）。

The French is telling on this point. When one employs an
adverb when one speaks, when one says real-ly, mental-ly,
heroic-ly, adding on this -ly already in itself tells you that one is
ly-ing. There is some­ thing of a lie in any adverb. This is no
accident. When we interpret, we must pay attention to this.



- 25 -


在这一点上，法语很有说服力。当一个人说话时使用
副词，当他说real-ly，mental-ly，heroic-ly 时，加了这个-ly
本身就在告诉你，他在撒谎。任何副词都有一些谎言的成
分。这不是偶然，我们在诠释（interpret）时必须注意到这
一点。

Someone who is fairly close to me made the remark with
regard to the tongue, in so far as this designates the instrument
of speech,that it is also the tongue that carries what are called
taste buds. Well, I retorted that ce qu'on dit ment is not for
nothing.

有一个和我关系相当好的人做了些关于语言（tongue）
的评论，某种程度上它是指说话的工具（也就是舌头），但
舌头上也有所谓的味蕾。好吧，我反驳说，“我们所说的谎
言（ce qu'on dit ment）”7这句话不是白说的。

It's good of you to chuckle, but it's not funny because when
all is said and done the equivoque is all we have as a weapon
against the symptom.

7 Ce qu'on dit ment，“我们所说的谎言”，与ce condiment（调味品）同音。



- 26 -


窃笑是件好事，但这并不好笑，因为说来说去，双关
是我们对付症状的所有武器。

It so happens that I afford myself the luxury of supervising,
as they call it, a certain number of people who have authorized
themselves, each on their own, to be analysts, in keeping with
my formula. There are two stages. There is a stage when they go
like a rhino. They go barging in any old how, and I always go
along with them. Indeed, they are always right. The second stage
consists in playing on the equivoque that might free up
something of the sinthome.

碰巧的是，我有能力进行他们所谓的监督，监督一定
数量的人，这些人按照我的公式去自己做分析师，每个人
都是自己的分析师。这有两个阶段。有一个阶段，他们像
一头犀牛一样前进。他们随便乱闯，而我也总是跟着他们
走。事实上，他们总是对的。第二个阶段是玩弄双关，这
可能会释放出一些来源于圣状的事物。

Indeed, interpretation operates solely through equivoques.
There has to be something in the signifier that resonates.



- 27 -


事实上，解释完全是通过双关来运作的。在有能指中
不得不有一些东西能引起共鸣。

It comes as a surprise that this wasn't even remotely
apparent to the British philosophers. I'm calling them
philosophers because they're not psychoanalysts. They staunchly
believe that speech doesn't have any effects. They're wrong.
They do entertain the exist­ ence of drives, but even then, only
when they are good enough not to translate Trieb as instinct.
They don't entertain the idea that the drives are the echo in the
body of a fact of saying.

令人惊讶的是，这对英国哲学家来说甚至一点也不明
显。我称他们为哲学家是因为他们不是精神分析学家。他
们坚定地认为言说不具有任何效应。他们错了。他们确实
接受了驱力的存在，但即使如此，也只是在他们足够聪明
不把驱力（Trieb）翻译成本能（instinct）的时候。他们不
接受这样的观念，即驱力是对言说的事实在身体中的回声。

For this fact of saying to resonate, for it to be consonant, to
use another word of sinthome madaquin, the body has to be



- 28 -

sensitive to it. And it's a fact that it is. The body is sensitive to it
because it has a few orifices, the most important of which is the
ear because it can't be sealed, shut, or closed off. It is because of
this that there is a response in the body to what I have called the
voice.

为了让这个言说的事实产生共鸣，为了让它产生协调
的和声，我用另一个词sinthome madaquin，因而身体不得
不对它感到敏感。而这是一个事实。身体对它感到敏感是
因为它有几个孔，其中最重要的是耳朵，因为它不能被密
封、关闭或封闭。正是因为如此，身体对我所说的声音有
一种反应。

The trouble is surely that there is not only the ear. The gaze
also puts up some stiff competition.
More geometrico. Because of form, the form that was so
dear to Plato, the individual presents himself just as he has been
put together, as a body. And this body possesses such a power of
captivation that up to a certain point one should be envious of
the blind. How might a blind man, assuming he knows how to
use braille, read Euclid?




- 29 -

麻烦的是，肯定不仅是耳朵。凝视也引出了一些激烈
的竞争。
更多的是几何学上的竞争。由于形式，即柏拉图所珍
视的形式，个体就像被放在一起一般，将自身作为一个身
体呈现出来。而这个身体拥有如此强大的捕捉能力，以至
于在某一点上，人们应该羡慕盲人。假设一个盲人知道如
何使用盲文，他何以阅读欧几里得？

The astonishing thing is that form offers up nothing more
than the bag, or, if you like, the bubble, because it's something
that inflates.

令人惊讶的是，形式提供的不过是袋子，或者，如果
你愿意，（也可以说）是气泡，因为它是可以膨胀的东西。

The obsessional is more stricken by this than anyone else
because, as I said somewhere and was reminded recently, he
belongs to the same register as the frog who wanted to be as big
as an ox. A certain fable allows us to see the effects of that. It's
particularly difficult, as we know, to wrench the obsessional
away from the stranglehold of the gaze.




- 30 -

强迫症患者比其他人更受打击，正如我在什么地方说
过的，最近有人提醒我，他和想变成牛一样大的青蛙8注册
进了同一个系统中。某个寓言故事让我们看到了它的影响。
正如我们所知，要把强迫症患者从凝视的束缚中解脱出来
是特别困难的。

The bag, such as it is envisaged in the set theory that
Cantor founded, is made manifest, or even demonstrated - if
every dem­ onstration is to be held to demonstrate the imaginary
that it implies - as fit to be connoted with an ambiguity between
1 and 0. This is the only fitting support for the confines of the
empty set, which imposes itself in this theory. Hence our
scription S1 which, I specify, is read S subscript 1. It does not
form the unity of one, but it indicates that it is an empty bag,
since it is able to contain nothing.

这个袋子，如康托尔建立的集合理论中所设想的，很
明显，甚至被证明了——如果每个过程都被证明它所暗示

8 出自拉封丹寓言。原文如下：一只青蛙见到一头牛，十分羡慕牛的大块头。
自己全身不比鸡蛋大,妒火中烧愁又愁。它躺在地上鼓着气,不撑成牛样不罢休。
它一边使劲一边问,叫另一只青蛙好好瞅：“好姐姐,看清楚我的个头够不够?告诉
我,差多少,难道我还远不足? “对得很,远不足。”“你看现在怎么样?”“差得远,远
不够。”“你看现在怎么样?“远不够,远不足。”就是如此这般地鼓呀鼓,小小青蛙
最终撑破了肚。世界上不乏这种人他们不比青蛙更高明。乡绅仿效贵族盖宫廷
夜郎之国到处派大使,侯爵身边也要伴侍童。



- 31 -

的想象——如同类似于意味着1 和0 之间的模糊性。这是对
限制空集唯一合理的支持，它在这个理论中对它自身施加
了影响。因此，我指定符号S1读作S 下标1（ S subscript 1）。
它不构成统一体，但它表明它是一个空袋子，因为它什么
也不能装。

It none the less remains that an empty bag is still a bag,
namely the one that can be imagined only on the basis of the ex-
sistence and the consistence that the body possesses on account
of being a pot. This ex-sistence and this consistence have to be
held to be real, since the real is the fact of holding them together,
hence the word Begriff, which means precisely that.

尽管如此，一个空袋子仍然是一个袋子，也就是说，
由于成为了一个容器使得身体得以被占据，此者只能基于
外—实存（ex-sistence）和一致性被想象。这种外—实存和
一致性不得不被坚持认为是实在的，因为实在是把它们联
系在一起的事实，因而有了“概念（Begriff）”，而这个词指
的就是这个意思。

The imaginary here shows its homogeneity with the real,
and it shows that this homogeneity is due solely to the fact of



- 32 -

number, in so far as number is binary, 1 or 0. This means that it
only supports 2 by dint of 1 not being 0, by dint of 1 ex-sisting
relative to 0, but it doesn't consist in any of it.

想象在这里显示了它与实在的同质性，并且它显示了
这种同质性完全是基于数字的事实，即就二进制数字而言：
1 或0。这意味着，仅仅由于它是1 而非0 它才支持2，由
于1 相对于0 是外—实存的，但它并不包含其中的任何内容。

So it is that Cantor's theory has to start afresh from the pair,
but then the set stands in third place. The conjunction between
the first set and what amounts to the Other doesn't come about.
This is pre­ cisely why the symbol offloads onto the imaginary.

因此，康托尔的理论必须从这对组合重新开始，但这
对组合却排在第三位。第一个集合与相当于他者的事物的
结合并没有出现。这也正是为什么符号会卸载（offload）到
想象（界）。

The symbol carries the subscript 2, indicating that it is a
pair, that is to say, that it introduces division in the subject,
whoever this subject might be, through whatever is uttered here



- 33 -

de facto. Since this fact remains suspended from the enigma of
enunciation, which is simply a fact closed in upon itself - the
fact of the fact, as it is written, lefaite du fait, the pinnacle of the
fact, or lefait du faite, the fact of the pinnacle, as it is said. The
facts are equal in fact. Equivocal and equivalent, the fact is
thereby the limit of what is said.

这个符号带有下标2（S2），用以表明它是一对，也就
是说，它在主体中引入了分裂，不管这个主体是谁，不管
通过什么东西在此被说出了事实。由于这个事实仍然悬停
在发音的谜团上，它只是一个封闭在自己身上的事实——
事实的事实，正如它所被写出的，le faîte du fait，事实的顶
峰，或le fait du faîte，顶峰的事实，正如它所被说出的。事
实是平等的事实。模棱两可和相当的，事实由此成为所被
说出的事物的极限。

What is incredible is that men saw quite clearly that the
symbol could only be a fractured part, and that this has been so,
as it were, since time immemorial. But what is also incredible is
that they didn't see at the time, the time of this immemorial time,
that this entailed the unity and the reciprocity of the signifier
and the signified - and that consequently the originary signified



- 34 -

doesn't mean anything, that it is only a sign of arbitration
between two signifiers - a sign of arbitration and, due to this fact,
there is no arbitrariness in choosing between them.

不可思议的是，人们很清楚地看到，象征这只能是一
个断裂的部分，而且自古以来就是如此。但同样令人难以
置信的是，他们当时没有看到，自古以来，这就意味着能
指和所指的统一性和（unity）相互性（reciprocity）——因
此，原初能指不意味着什么，它只是两个能指之间一个仲
裁的标志，因此，在它们之间没有任意性的选择。

Arbitration or, to say it in English, umpire. That's how
Joyce writes it. There is only any umpire on the basis of empire,
on the basis of the imperium over the body, just as everything
bears its mark following the trial by ordeal.

仲裁（Arbitration），或者用英语说，umpire。乔伊斯就
是这样写的。只有在帝国的基础上，基于对身体的统治，
才有仲裁，就像一切都在神明的审判后留下了印记。

Here the 1 confirms its detachment from the 2. It only
makes 3 through imaginary forcing, the same that imposes a



- 35 -

kind of will that suggests to one to manhandle the other, without
being bound to either of them.

在这里，１确认了自身与２的分离。它只是由于想象
中的强迫（imaginary forcing）创造了３，也就是强加一种
意志，让其中一个去虐待（manhandle）另一个，而它不受
二者中任意一个的束缚。

3

For the condition to have been expressly posited that,
starting with three rings, a link is made such that a break in one
of the rings, the middle one, if I may put it in a somewhat
abridged form, sets the other two, whichever they may be, free
from one another, it had to be noticed that this had been
inscribed on the coat of arms of the Borromeo family.

因为条件已经明确假设：从三个环开始，一个链环是
这样打破其中一个环的，也就是中间的那个环，如果我可
以把它放在稍微被删减的形式中，设置其他两个，无论哪
个都可以从另一个中解脱出来，必须注意，这些已经被刻
在波罗米家族的徽章上了。



- 36 -


The so-called noeud borroméen was thus already there
without anyone being shrewd enough to learn a lesson from it.

因此，所谓的波罗米结（noeud borroméen）已经存在，
而没有人敏锐到从中获得启发。

It is precisely here that we find what lies behind the error of
thinking that this knot is a norm for the relationship between the
three functions that only exist one relative to the other in their
implementation within the being who, forming a knot, believes
himself to be a man. It is not a break between the symbolic, the
imaginary and the real that defines perversion, it is that they
already stand apart in such a way that a fourth term has to be
supposed.. which on this occasion is the sinthome.

正是在这里，我们发现在思考的错误的背后：即认为



- 37 -

这个结是三种功能之间关系的规范，而这三种功能只能在
关联其它两者的情况下形成结，这一情况基于认为自己是
构成结的那个存在，同时相信他自身成为了一个男人。这
并不是象征、想象和实在之间的断裂定义了倒错
（perversion），而是它们已经以这样一种方式分离，以至于
必须要有第四个术语，而它就是圣状（sinthome）。

I'm saying that what forms the Borromean link has to be
sup­posed to be tetradic - that perversion merely means version
vers père, a version towards the father — and that all in all, the
father is a symptom, or a sinthome, as you wish. The ex-sistence
of the symptom is what is im-ply-cated by the very position that
presupposes this enigmatic bond between the imaginary, the
symbolic, and the real.

我是说，形成波罗米结的东西必须得有四项，倒错仅
仅意味着针对父亲的一个倒转（version vers le père），总而
言之，父亲是一种症状，或者说是一种圣状，随你怎么想。
症状的实存是通过特定的位置而被牵连的事物，这一特定
位置预设了这个位于想象、象征和实在之间的神秘的结。




- 38 -


The Borromean link
波罗米结

The following figuration, here on the left, is a diagram of
the imaginary, the symbolic, and the real, each separate from the
other. Already, in my previous planar diagrams, you have the
possibility of binding them together. With what? With the
sinthome.. the fourth ring.

下面的图式，位于左边的，是一个想象、象征和实在
的图式，每个都是独立于他者的。在我之前的二维图示中，
你们有可能已经将它们结合在一起了。用什么？用 “圣状”，
也就是第四环。




- 39 -


The three separate rings (left);
the rings linked by the fourth ring, the sinthome (right)

三个独立的环（左图）；
通过第四个环连接起来的环，即Sinthome（右图）

That gives you this relationship, namely, the imaginary, the
real the symptom - which I'm writing here with a sigma - and
the symbolic. Each one is interchangeable in a way that will, I
hope, strike you as quite straightforward. To spell it out, 1 onto 2
can permute to 2 onto I" while 3 onto 4 can permute to 4 onto 3.

这一（第四环）给了你这种关系，即想象和实在的症
状——我在这里写下一个sigma（∑）和符号（symbolic, S）。
每一个都是可以互换的，我希望这将使你感到非常直观。
说白了，"1 到2 "可以互换成 "2 到1"，而 "3 到4 "可以互换



- 40 -

成 "4 到3"。


The combinatory I R ∑ S

组合的I R ∑ S

We find ourselves in the situation where the link of 1 onto
2 indeed of 2 onto 1, has in the midst of it, as it were, the 3 and
the 4, that is, the ∑ and the S. In what way do the symptom and
the symboi find themselves caught between the real and the
imaginary? I'll show you with this simple configuration.

我们发现自己处于这样一种情况：1 与2 的链环，实际
上是2 与1 的链环，在它中间有3 和4，也就是∑和S。症



- 41 -

状和符号以何种方式发现它们自身在实在和想象中被俘获？
我将用这个简单的构造来告诉你们。

On the Logical Use of the Sinthome

论圣状的逻辑运作

The four-component Borromean link, showing the symptom and
the symbol between the real and the imaginary

四组分波罗米结，显示了实在与想象之间的症状和符号

On the left, you can see four bights being pulled by the
capital R ring, while on the right the I ring commingles with
them in a certain way by passing over the symbol and under the
symptom. In other words, the two in the middle - the symptom
and the symbol — present themselves in such a way that one of



- 42 -

the far-end terms takes them up in their entirety, while the other
far-end term passes over the one that is on top and under the one
that is underneath. It is in this form that the bond I expressed
through the opposition between Rand I presents itself.

在左边，你们可以看到四个词被大写的R 环拉着，而
在右边，I 环以某种方式与它们结合在一起，从符号上方和
症状下方穿过。换句话说，中间的两个——症状和符号—
—以这样一种方式呈现出来，其中远端的一项将它们全部
占据，而另一个远端的项则从上面的那个项上经过，并位
于下面的那个项之下。正是基于这种形式，I 结通过R 与I
之间的所表达的对立将其自身呈现了出来。

I shall also add a different figure, a symmetrical one, which
you regularly get when trying to make a four-component
Borromean knot.

我还将添加一个不同的数字，一个对称的数字，当你
试图制作一个四组分的波罗米结时，你将经常得到这样的
数字。




- 43 -


Symmetrical figuration of the four-component Borromean link

四组分的波罗米结的对称性图式

The Oedipus complex is, as such, a symptom. Everything is
sustained in so far as the Name-of-the-Father is also the Father
of the name, which doesn’ t make the symptom any the less
necessary.

俄狄浦斯情结本身就是一种症状。只要 “父之名 “同时
也是 “名之父”，那么一切都将得以维持。这并不意味着这
种症状就不那么必要了。

The Other at issue manifests itself in Joyce by the fact that,
all in all, he is tasked with a father. To the extent that he must
support this father for him to subsist, as is borne out by Ulysses,
Joyce, through his art – this art which is always that which ,



- 44 -

down through the age comes to us as emanating from the artisan
-not only makes his family subsist, but illustriates it, as it were.
By the same token he illustriates what somewhere he calls my
country, or better still, the uncreated conscience of my race, on
which he ends A Portrait of the Artist. This is the mission Joyce
sets himself.

这一问题中的大他者在乔伊斯身上将其自身表现为，
总而言之，他被赋予了一个父亲的任务。正如通过《尤利
西斯》所生成的那样，在一定程度上他必须供养这一“父亲”
的生计，乔伊斯通过他的艺术——这种艺术始终是时代向
我们展现的工匠的艺术——不仅供养他的家庭也正如它所
是地光耀门楣（illustriates it）。同样地，他也给他称之为
“我的国家 ”的东西涨了脸面（illustriates），或者更恰当地说
是：“我的种族所未创造的良心”，他在《艺术家的肖像》的
结尾中这样说道。这就是乔伊斯为自己设定的使命。

In this sense, I am hereby announcing what this year shall
be my examination of art.

在这个意义上，我在此宣布，今年我将进行何种艺术
考察。



- 45 -


In what way is artifice expressly able to target what
presents itself in the first instance as a symptom? In what way
can art – the artisanal­ foil, as it were, what imposes as a
symptom? Namely, truth.

诡计以何种方式明确地能够将如下事物作为目标：即
它自身在作为症状的第一个例证中所呈现出的东西？艺术
——工匠精神的衬托，以何种方式如其所是地作为症状施
加影响？（这一问题的答案）也就是，真理。

4

I included truth in my two tetrahedrons.

我这两个四面体中蕴藏了真相。




- 46 -

Tetrahedral schemas of the discourse of the master

主人话语的四面体图示

Where is the truth in this setting? I said that in the
discourse of the master it stood as something that is supposed in
the subject, barred S. In so far as he is divided, the subject is still
subject to the fantasy. It is there, at barred S, at the level of truth,
that we must consider the half-saying.

这一的图示的真相在哪里呢？我说过，在主人话语中，
S/ （划斜杠的S）是主体中假定存在的东西，就它的分裂而
言，主体仍然受制于幻想。正是在那里，在S/（划斜杠的S）
处，在真相的层面，我们必须考虑半说。

At this stage, the subject can only represent himself with
the signifier subscript 1, S1. As for the signifier subscript 2, S2,



- 47 -

there stands the artisan, in so far as through the conjunction of
two signifiers he is capable of producing what I have called the
object a.

在这一阶段，主体只能用S1 来表示他自身。至于S2，
则是工匠（artisan），两个符号结合就能产生我所说的对象a。

Earlier I illustrated this by the relationship with the ear and
with the eye, even evoking the closed mouth. I have offered a
figuration of division with the duplicity of the symbol and the
symptom.

早些时候，我通过耳朵与眼睛的关系说明了这一点，
甚至提到了紧闭的嘴。我已经提供了一个对象征和症状的
双重性进行了划分的图示。

In so far as the discourse of the master reigns, the S2 is
divided. The division at issue is that between the symbol and the
symptom.

就主人话语而言，S2 是分裂的，象征和症状之间的划
分还尚存争议。



- 48 -


This division is reflected, so to speak, in the subject's
division. It is because the subject is what one signifier represents
relative to another signifier that we are required by its insistence
to show that it is in the symptom that one of these two signifiers
from the symbolic derives its support.

可以说，这种划分反映在（对）主体的划分上。正是
因为主体是一个能指相对于另一个能指所代表的东西，所
以我们通过它的坚持被要求展示如下内容：即正是在症状
中这两个能指中的一个从符号中得到了它的支持。

In this sense, in the symptom's articulation with the
symbol.. there is, I shall say, merely a false hole.

在这个意义上，在症状与象征的衔接中......我要说的是，
这仅仅是一个虚假的洞。

Supposing the consistency of any one of these function ­
symbolic, imaginary or real - to form a circle presupposes a hole.
But something else is at stake in the case of the symbol and the
symptom.



- 49 -


假设任何这些作用的一致性——象征、想象或实在—
—形成一个预设了一个洞的环。但是，在象征和症状的情
况下，还有其他的东西处于失败的边缘。


The false hole of the symbol and the symptom

象征的假洞和症状

What makes a hole here is the full length of one circle
folded over the full folded length of the other. But this is a false
hole. For us to have something that might be qualified as a true
hole, we have to surround, to enclose, one of the circles with a
consistence that make them hold together, which looks like an
air cavity, what in topology we call a torus. Pierre Soury - to
cite him by name, I don't know if he's here today - has set this
out rather well.




- 50 -

这里两个完全拉长的环折叠在一起组成了一个洞。但
这是一个虚假的洞。为了得到可能被认定为真正的洞，我
们必须把其中一个环用一致性环绕并封闭起来，以便使它
们串在一起，这看起来像一个空腔，在拓扑学中我们称之
为圆环面（torus）。皮埃尔·苏里（Pierre Soury）——提起他
的名字，我不知道他今天是否在这里——已经很好地阐述
了这一点。


A true hole obtained from a false hole by enclosing one of the
folded circle

通过包围其中一个折叠的圆环从虚假洞中得到真正的洞

This also means that, for the hole to subsist, to be
maintained one has simply to imagine a straight line here. So
long as thi traight line stretches to infinity it will fulfil the same



- 51 -

role.

这也意味着，为了使洞的持续存在，人们只需想象这
里有一条直线。只要这条直线延伸得无限远，它也能发挥
同样的作用。


A true hole obtained by adding a straight line stretching to
infinity

增加一条无限延伸的直线得到的真正的洞

Over the course of the year we are going to have to look
again at what this infinity is. We are going to have to look again
at just what a straight line is, what makes it subsist, and in what
way it is akinas it were, to a circle.




- 52 -

在这一年的课程中，我们将要再次看看这个无限意味
着什么。我们必须再次审视什么是直线，是什么让它得以
持存，并且它以何种方式与一个环相似。

I will most certainly have to look again at the circle, too. It
has a function with which the police are very well acquainted.
The circle is used for circulation. It is in precisely this respect
that the police possess a support that's been around for some
time. Hegel saw its function very clearly. For the police, it is
simply a matter of keeping things turning round. This form is
certainly not what is in question here.

当然我也要再次审视一下这个环。公安部门非常熟悉
它的其中一个功能。这个环是用来流通（circulation）的。
正是在这个方面，公安部门已经在一段时间内得到了某种
支持。黑格尔很清楚它的功能。对公安部门来说，这只是
一个保持事物不断转变的问题。环的这一形式当然不是我
们要讨论的问题。

That simply adding an infinite straight line to the false hole
should turn it into a hole that subsists in a Borromean fashion,
this is the fact on which I shall stop for today.



- 53 -


在虚假的洞上加一条无限的直线，把它变成一个以波
罗米样式存在的洞，我今天就在这个简单的事实上停下。

1975 年11 月18 日





- 54 -

II
ON WHAT MAKES A HOLE IN THE REAL
什么在实在中开了一个洞？
The fourth ring
A geometry forbidden to the imaginary
Meeting Chomsky
No hope of escaping from feeble-mindedness

第四个环
禁止想象之状
会见乔姆斯基

一种能使症状具体化的艺术



- 55 -



Figuration of the four-component link

四组分链结的图示

It can't go on like this. There are too many of you.

不能再继续这样了。你们人太多了

Well, I still hope to get from you what I got from the audi
ence over in the United States, where I have just spent a busy
fortnight.




- 56 -

行吧，我仍然希望你们能从我这学到我在美国听众那
里得到的东西，我刚在这上面忙了两星期

I was able to take in a certain number of things over there,
and in particular, if I understood correctly, a certain weariness
that is being felt principally by the analysts.

我在那里了解了一些情况，特别是，如果我理解正确
的话，是主要由分析师们感受到的某种疲惫感。

I can only say, good gracious, that I was treated
exceedingly well, which is not to say very much, is it? To use a
term which is the same one I use for the situation of mankind, I
felt I was being more or less drawn in, or, if you would care to
hear this, I was sucked in - sucked into a kind of whirlwind that
only finds some ready response in what I have been bringing to
the fore with my knot.

我只能说，天哪，我受到了非常好的待遇，这并不能
说明什么，不是吗？用我对人类处境使用的一个术语来说，
我觉得我或多或少地被吸引了，或者，如果你愿意听的话，
我被吸进去了——被吸进一种漩涡中，而只有在我的结中



- 57 -

才能找到对此的回应。

1

As those of you who've been here for a while now have
been able to see, well, to hear, it is not by chance, but rather
little by little, step by step, that I have come round to expressing
by means of the function of the knot what I initially put forward
as the triple alliance of the symbolic, the imaginary, and the real.

正如你们中那些已经在这里呆了一段时间的人所看到
的，嗯，听到的，这不是偶然的，而是一点一点的，一步
一步的，我已经开始通过结的功能来表达我最初提出的象
征、想象和实在三者连结的东西。

The knot is formed in the spirit of a new mos geometricus.
Mosmeans mode or manner. Initially, we are always captivated
by a kind of geometry that last time I qualified as being
comparable with the bag, that is, the surface.

这个结是以新的几何学原理（mos geometricus）的精神
形成的。Mos 的意思是模式或方式。最初，我们总是被一



- 58 -

种几何学所吸引，上次我把这种几何学与包相提并论，也
就是表面。

Pondering commonly happens with eyes shut. When one
ponders the knot - you can have a go at it, it's very hard - one
can make neither head nor tail of it. So I'm not really sure that
I've got this one right for you, though it does seem so to my eye.

思考通常是闭着眼睛进行的。当一个人在思考这个结
时——你可以试一试，这很难——一个人既难以想象它的
头，也难以想象它的尾。所以我不太确定我是否为你找到
了正确的答案，尽管在我看来确实如此。

No, it looks like there's a mistake. In fact, yes, there's a
mistake here. There you go, because this part here needs to be
removed.

不，看起来好像有一个错误。事实上，没错，这里有
一个错误。这里的这部分需要被删除。

This four-component knot is constructed using this, which
you're quite familiar with. You have this shape in a Borromean



- 59 -

knot.

他的四组分结是用这个构造的，你对这个很熟悉。你
在波罗米结中有这种形状。


Folded shape from the four-component link

四组分链结的折叠形状

As it happens, this is duplicated.




- 60 -

碰巧的是，这一点是重复的。


Double folded shape

双重折叠形状

Then you have to finish it off with two further rings.

然后你必须用另外两个环来完成它。




- 61 -


Second figuration of the four-component link

四组分链结的第二种图示

There is another way of drawing and duplicating the folded
shape, making the two folds hook onto one another.

还有一种方法是把这个折叠的形状移动并重复，使两
个折叠处相互勾连。




- 62 -


Another figuration of the double folded shape

双重折叠形状的另一种图示

And there is a third way, which consists in employing this,
which I happen to have already shown you once, and which in
and of itself constitutes a closed circle.

还有第三种方法，就是采用这种方法，我碰巧已经给
你看过一次，它本身就构成了一个闭环。




- 63 -


Third figuration of the double folded shape

四组分链结的第三种图示

However, in the two shapes that I presented before, the two
circuits in the middle can be handled in such a way that they can
be set free from one another. This is why the two circles traced
out here in red can turn this into a Borromean knot, that is to say,
a knot in which sectioning any one of the circles will free the
others.

然而，在我之前介绍的两个形状中，中间的两个环可
以以这样的方式处理，即它们可以从彼此中解开出来。这
就是为什么这里用红色描出的两个环可以变成一个波罗米
结，也就是说，在这个结中，分割任何一个圆都可以解开



- 64 -

其他的圆。9

2

Analysis is, in sum, the reduction of initiation to its reality,
that is to say, to the fact that strictly speaking there is no
initiation. Every single subject in analysis reveals how he is
always and only ever a supposition.

总而言之，分析是将初始状态还原为现实，也就是说，
还原为严格来说没有初始状态的事实。分析中的每一个主
体都揭示了他如何永远只是一个假设。

None the less, experience demonstrates that this
supposition is always given over to what I shall call an
ambiguity. I mean that the subject as such is always, not only
double, but divided. It's a matter of accounting for what it is in
this division that makes for something real.

9 应该注意的是，拉康所说的“波罗米属性”更常对应于“Brunnian 属性”。在
这里和之后，当拉康说到一个链环“以波罗米方式”捆绑时，它等同于
Brunnian 链环的标准，即认为一个给定链环的最小切割数µ（即足以解除所有
剩余环的最小切割数）和最大切割数M（即可用于解除所有剩余环的最大切割
数）都等于1。例如，已经表明，使用更通常的波罗米属性的定义（μ=1，M>l），
在M=2 的情况下存在一个四组分的波罗米链环。拉康的定义与传统定义的一致
之处仅仅在于μ=1。



- 65 -


尽管如此，经验表明，这种假设总是被我称之为模棱
两可（ambiguity）的东西所笼罩。我的意思是，主体本身
总是，不仅是加倍的，而且是分裂的。这是一个需要说明
的问题，正是这种分裂，创造了实在（le Réel）。

On this point we have to come back to Freud, since he is
the one who was the great trailblazer in apprehending this.

在这一点上，我们必须回到弗洛伊德那里，因为他是
领悟到这一点的伟大开拓者。

On the back cover of Erich Fromm's latest book, which you
can easily get, if memory serves, from Gallimard, one can read
what is set out as la psychanalyse appréhendée à travers son
‘père’, psycho analysis apprehended through its 'father'. In what
way did Freud a bourgeois if I've read correctly, a bourgeois
brimming with prejudices, reach something that holds the
specific value of what he said, which is certainly no small matter,
since its aim was to say the truth about mankind?

在埃里希·弗洛姆（Erich Fromm）新书的封底，如果没



- 66 -

记错的话，你可以很容易地从Gallimard10买到这本书，人
们可以读到这样一段话：“la psychanalyse appréhendée à
travers son ‘père’，通过其‘父亲’理解的精神分析。”如果
我没看错的话，弗洛伊德是一个资产阶级，一个充满偏见
的资产阶级，他是以什么方式达到了他所说的具体价值的
呢，这当然不是小事，因为其目的是说出人类的真相？

To which I've added the fallowing rectification, which
entailed its share of blood, sweat and tears for me, that the only
truth is one that is said by half, just like the subject that it entails.
To express it as I stated it, truth can only be half-said.

在此基础上，我又做了如下的修正，这对我来说需要
付出一定的精力，即唯一的真理是只说了一半，就像它所
包含的主题一样。按照我的说法，真理只能被半说。

My starting point is my condition, which is that of bringing
to Man what the Scripture states, not as an help meet for him,
but as a help meet against him. I try to take my bearings from
this condition, and this is truly what led me, in a way that is

10 弗罗姆的《弗洛伊德的使命—人格与影响力分析》的法译本以La Mission de
Sigmund Freud: une analyse de sa personnalité et de son influence 为题，由Editions
Complexe 而非Gallimard 出版。



- 67 -

worth remarking upon, to considering the knot.

我从我的立场出发，也就是圣经中所说的那样——不
是带给人帮助来满足他们的需求，而是对他反抗自身的帮
助。我试着从这个立场出发来确定我的方向,正是这一点，
以一种值得评论的方式，引导我思考这个结。

This consideration is constituted by a geometry that may be
said to be forbidden to the imaginary, because it can only be
imagined through all kinds of resistances and, indeed,
difficulties. This is what the knot substantifies in so far as it is
Borromean.

这种思考是由一种可以说是对想象的禁止的几何构成
的，因为它只能通过各种阻力和困难来想象。这就是结的
本质，因为它是波罗米结。

One of the things that struck me most when I was in the US
was my meeting with Chomsky, which was altogether
intentional on my part. I was quite taken aback, and I told him
so.




- 68 -

当我在美国时，最让我印象深刻的事情之一是我与乔
姆斯基的会面，这完全是我有意的进行的会面。我大吃一
惊，并且我告诉了他。

What made me sense the great distance that lies between us
was that he simply affirmed to me, in person, one idea that I
realized was his own. I can't say that this idea is refutable,
because it's the most common place idea, but it does strike me as
a precarious one.

让我感觉到我们之间的巨大距离的是，他只是亲自向
我确认了我意识到的一个想法是他自己也有的。我不能说
这个想法是可以反驳的，因为它是最常见的想法，但它确
实给我留下了不确定的印象。

This idea starts off from the consideration of something
that presents itself as a body, conceived of as being equipped
with organs. This conception implies that the organ is a tool, a
tool for grasping or apprehending, and there is no objection in
principle to the tool apprehending itself as such.

这个想法是从对某物的考虑开始的，它呈现为一个被



- 69 -

认为是有器官的身体。这一概念意味着器官是一种工具，
一种用于抓取或理解的工具，原则上没有人反对这种工具
本身。

So it is that Chomsky considers lan guage, among other
things, to be determined by a genetic fact. In a word, language is
itself an organ. He said as much, right there in front of me, in
those very words.

因此，乔姆斯基认为语言是由基因决定的。总之，语
言本身就是一个器官。他就在我面前用这些话表达了这样
的意思。

It strikes me as altogether startling - this is what I was
expressing in saying that I was taken aback- that one should be
able to turn this language into a return upon itself as an organ.

这让我感到十分震惊——这就是我所说的，我感到震
惊——一个人应该能够将这种语言转变为一种器官的回归。

Indeed, to my mind, if one doesn't admit the principial truth
that language is tied to something that makes a hole in the real,



- 70 -

it is not simply difficult but impossible to consider how it is
handled.

事实上，在我看来，如果一个人不承认语言是与某种
东西联系在一起的这一原则性真理，那么要考虑如何处理
语言不仅是困难的，而且是不可能的。

Observational method cannot start off from language
without accepting the fact that, in what can be situated as real,
language only emerges in making a hole. It is on the basis of this
function of the hole that language effects its purchase on the real.

观察法不能从语言开始，而不接受这样一个事实，即
在可以被定位的实在中，语言只在制造一个洞时出现。正
是基于空洞的这一功能，语言才影响其在实在中的掌握
（prise）。

It's not easy for me to impress upon you the full weight of
this conviction. This is a conviction that to my eye is
unavoidable, given that truth as such is only possible when this
real is hollowed out. Moreover, language eats into the real.




- 71 -

我很难让你明白这一信念的全部分量。这是一个信念，
在我看来是不可避免的，因为只有当实在被掏空时，真理
才可能。此外，语言蚕食着实在。

To speak as Chomsky does, see if you will what this
genetic real is all about. Language only enables it to be
approached in terms of signs, in other words, messages.

要像乔姆斯基那样说，看看你是否会理解这种基因
（遗传）性的实在。语言只能通过符号，换句话说，通过
信息来接近它。

The molecular gene is reduced to what secured the
reputation of Crick and Watson, namely the double helix from
which the various tiers that organize the body at a number of
different levels are deemed to set off.

让克里克和沃森名声大噪的，是他们对基因分子还原
为双螺旋结构的发现，身体组织中多个不同方面上的各个
层次被认为都是从该结构出发的。

First there is division, development, and cellular



- 72 -

specialization. Next there is specialization based on hormones,
which act as vehicles for all sorts of messages that direct the
organic information.

首先是分裂、发展和细胞分化。其次是基于激素的专
门化，它充当各种信息的载体，引导有机信息。

With all these so-called messages, we arrive at a great
spiriting away of what the real actually involves.

通过所有这些所谓的信息，我们对实在所涉及的东西
有了一个巨大的启发。

This is still, however, nothing more than a veil that has
been drawn over what lies behind the efficacy of language, that
is, over the fact that language is not in itself a message, but
rather is only sustained by the function of what I've called the
hole in the real.

然而，这只不过是一层面纱，遮住了语言的效力背后
的东西，也就是说，语言本身并不是一种信息，而是由我
所说的“实在中的洞”的功能支撑的。



- 73 -


For this, there exists the path of our new mos geometricus,
that is, the substance that results from the specific efficacy of
language, and which is supported by the function of the hole.

因此，存在我们新的几何体原理的路径，即由语言的
特定效力所产生的实体，并由空洞的功能所支撑。

To express this in the terms of the much-vaunted
Borromean knot that I've been relying on, let's say that it hinges
entirely on the equivalence between a circle and a straight line
stretching to infinity.

为了用我一直依赖并大肆吹嘘的波罗米结来表达这一
点，让我们先假设它完全取决于环和延伸到无穷远的直线
之间的等价性。

3

The diagram of the Borromean knot is this one, here on the
left. It is just as Borromean as my usual drawing, here on the
right.



- 74 -


波罗米结的示意图是这个，在左边。右边的则是我平
常画的波罗米结。



Two figurations of the Borromean link

波罗米链结的两种图示11

This is also true of the following drawing.

下面画的也是。


11 严格来说，左边由直线组成的图不是一个（波罗米）链环，因为在假设（蓝
色和绿色）线是无限的情况下，它遗漏了第六个交叉点，而这个交叉点将说明
它们联系的性质。这一点也适用于拉康随后所有使用直线的图示。



- 75 -


A further figuration of the Borromean link

进一步的波罗米链结图示

Thus it emerges that you get the same Borromean knot
when you replace the three circles with this one circle together
with this pair of straight lines that we suppose stretch to infinity.

因此，当你把三个环换成一个环再加上一对我们假设
延伸到无穷远的直线，你就得到了同样的波罗米结。

To account correctly for the Borromean knot it should be
emphasized that it is on the basis of three that a requirement
originates. The cipher 3 is the threshold, if I may say so, of the
specific requirement of the knot.




- 76 -

为了正确解释波罗米结，需要强调的是，在三环的基
础上产生了一个要求。如果我可以这么说的话，数字3 是结
的特定要求的起点（threshold）。12

It is possible, through a perfectly straightforward
manipulation, to make these three infinite straight lines run
parallel. To achieve this you would just have to make the faided
circle, the red one, more pliable.

通过完全直接的操作，可以使这三条无限直线平行运
行。要实现这一点，你只需要使这个环，红色的这个环，
变得更可塑（pliable）。


Parallel straight lines stretching to infinity

延伸到无穷远的平行直线

12 在这里和之后，当拉康使用chiffre（数字、密码）（更常规地被译为“数字
[number]”或“（数字的）图形[(numerical) figure]”时，我们选择了它的英语同
源词cipher（密码、暗号），以对应数字(number)的加密的隐喻性概念。



- 77 -


It is starting from the 3 that we have to define what is at
stake in the straight line's point at infinity. This is what allows us
on no account to make any mistake about what we may call their
concen tricity. Let's place the three points at infinity, which
complete the straight lines, here.

正是从3 开始，我们必须定义直线在无穷远处的点的关
键。这就是为什么我们绝不能在我们所谓的同心度上犯任
何错误。让我们把三个点放在无穷远处，在这里形成直线。


Infinite straight lines completed by their points at infinity

由无穷远点完成的无限直线

We can also permute these positions and make it so that
this first infinite line envelops the others instead of being



- 78 -

enveloped by them. The characteristic of this point at infinity is
that it cannot be situated, as one might express oneself, on any
side.

我们也可以置换这些位置，使第一条无限直线包围其
他位置，而不是被它们包围。这一在无穷远处的点的特点
是，正如人们可能表达的那样，它不能位于任何一边。

However, in starting off from 3, to figure it in this imaged
fashion, you have the following requirement. You can sense that
there are reasons why I had to trace these circles out in different
colours,whereas just before I was drawing the three straight
lines, completed by their points at infinity, in red.

然而，从3 开始，以这种想象的方式计算，你有以下要
求。你可以感觉到为什么我必须用不同的颜色画出这些圆
环，而就在我之前，我用红色画了三条直线，由它们在无
穷远处的点组成。




- 79 -


A further figuration of the Borromean link

进一步的波罗米链结图示

Not one of these circles, despite being enveloped by one of
the two others, fails to envelop the third. This is what constitutes
the proprety of the Borromean knot, a property with which I've
acquainted you on several occasions now. In this third
dimension, as it were, the Borromean knot consists in this
relationship which means that what is enveloped with respect to
one of the circles finds itself enveloping with respect to the
other one.

这些环中没有一个被另外两个环中的一个包围，但没
有一个不包围第三个。这就是波罗米结的属性，我已经在
很多场合认识了这个属性。可以说，在这个第三维度中，



- 80 -

波罗米结存在于这个关系中，这意味着被其中一个环包围
的环发现自己包围了另一个环。

It is in this respect that the following figure is exemplary,
which you usually see in the form of the armillary sphere, which
is used for sextants. To draw it out clearly, the blue circle will
always swing in the following way around the circle I've drawn
in green, while the red circle, in keeping with the inter-axial
swing, has to be like this.

正是在这一点上，下图是典型的，你通常可以在用于
六分仪的浑天仪中看到。为了清楚地画出来，蓝色圆环总
是以下面的方式绕着我画的绿色圆环摆动，而为了与轴间
摆动保持一致，红色圆环必须是这样的。


Diagram of the armillary sphere



- 81 -


浑天仪图示

There is a difference between this arrangement, which is
ordinary in any manipulation of the armillary sphere, and the
following arrangement.

这种布置与下面的布置之间存在差异，上面这种布置
在任何对浑天仪的布置中都是常见的。


Faulty Borromean link

错误的波罗米结

I'm redrawing it because this drawing is wrong. You can
see how the green circle is situated in relation to the blue circle
and the red circle. The blue circle, which here looks like it's the



- 82 -

middle one, cannot swing around, because it envelops the red
circle but is in turn enveloped by the green circle.

我正在重画，因为这张画错了。你可以看到绿色圆环
相对于蓝色圆环和红色圆环的位置。蓝色的圆环，这里看
起来像是中间的那个，不能旋转，因为它包围了红色的圆
环，但又被绿色的圆环包围。


Correct Borromean link

正确的波罗米链结

Even my hesitations are significant here. They bring out the
clumsiness with which the Borromean knot, which is the
exemplar of the knot, is necessarily handled.

甚至我的犹豫在这里也很重要。因为这展现了作为结



- 83 -

的典型的波罗米结在处理时是多么麻烦。

The fundamental character of this use of the knot is to
illustrate the triplicity that results from a consistence that is
assigned only on the basis of the imaginary, from a fundamental
hole that emerges from the symbolic, and from an ex-sistence
which belongs to the real, which is even its fundamental
character.

这种结的使用是为了说明三重性，三重性是由仅基于
想象的一致性、从符号中出现的基本空洞以及从属于实在
的外—实存中产生的，这甚至是它们的基本特征。

This method, because it does indeed involve method,
presents itself as something hopeless. There is no hope of
breaking, in any way whatsoever, the constitutive knot of the
symbolic, the imaginary, and the real.

这种方法（因为它确实涉及方法），呈现出无望的样子。
无论如何，没有希望打破象征、想象和实在的构成结。

In this respect, let's put it in the most coherent way we can,



- 84 -

it passes up what constitutes a virtue, a virtue that is even said to
be theological. This is why our analytic appreciation of what is
involved in the knot is the negative of religion.

在这方面，让我们用最连贯的方式来表达，它忽略了
什么是美德，一种我甚至说是神学的美德。这就是为什么
我们对这个结所涉及的东西的分析性理解是对宗教的否定。

We no longer believe in the object as such. It is in this
respect that I reject that the object can be grasped by any organ.

我们不再相信客体本身。正是在这方面，我反对任何
的被器官把握的客体。

When an organ is itself perceived as a tool, a tool that
stands apart, it is thereby being conceived of as an object. In
Chomsky's conception the object is not in itself broached except
through an object.

当一个器官本身被视为一个工具，一个独立的工具时，
它就被认为是一个客体。在乔姆斯基的概念中，除非通过
一个客体，否则客体本身不是自在地被引入的。



- 85 -


On the other hand, it is through the restitution of the subject
as such, inasmuch as the subject can only be divided by the
opera tion of language, that analysis finds the scope of its
diffusion.

另一方面，正是通过主体本身的偿还——因为主体只
能通过语言的操作来划分——分析才能找到其扩散的范围
（scope of its diffusion）。

Analysis finds the scope of its diffusion in the fact that it
calls into question science per se - science in so far as it makes
an object into a subject, whereas it is the subject that is divided
on his own account.

分析发现，它的扩散范围在于，它质疑科学，因为它
使一个客体成为一个主体，一个因其自身原因而被划分的
主体。

We don't believe in the object, but we observe desire, and
from this observation of desire we induce the cause as
something objectivized.



- 86 -


我们不相信客体，但我们观察欲望，通过对欲望的观
察，我们将原因归纳为某种客体化的东西。

The desire to take cognizance meets obstacles. I invented
the knot to embody such an obstacle. We need to become more
accustomed to this knot. I mean that the knot alone is the
conceivable support of a relationship between any one thing and
any other thing.

获得认可的愿望遇到了障碍。我发明了这个结来体现
这种障碍。我们需要更加习惯这个结。我的意思是，结本
身就是任何一个事物和任何其他事物之间关系的可想象的
支撑。

Although on one hand the knot is abstract, it must none the
less be thought of and conceived of as something concrete.

虽然一方面这个结是抽象的，但它必须被视为具体的
东西。

In which respect, although today you can see that I'm really



- 87 -

rather weary after my test in the US, I was nevertheless
rewarded, as I told you, because I was able to stir up what I will
call some agitation, some emotion, with these figures.

在这方面，虽然今天你可以看到我在美国的考查后真
的很疲惫，但正如我告诉你的那样，我还是得到了回报，
因为我能够用这些数字激起我所说的一些激动，一些情绪。

You can see these figures here, more or less substantialized
on account of being set down in written form, on account of
being drawn. This doesn't prevent the sentiment of the mental,
the sentimental, from being feeble-minded, because it can
always be reduced from some angle or other to the imaginary.

你可以在这里看到这些数字，由于以书面形式记录下
来，由于被绘制出来，或多或少地被实体化了。这并不能
阻止精神上的情感，多愁善感的，变得脆弱，因为它总是
可以从某个角度或其他角度还原到想象中。

The imagining of consistency makes straight for the
impossible dimension of the fracture, but it is in this respect that
a fracture can always be the real, the real as impossible. It is no



- 88 -

less compatible with the said imagining, and even constitutes it.

一致性的想象直接说明了断裂的不可能的维度，但正
是在这一点上，断裂总是实在的，不可能的实在 。它与所
述的想象同样兼容，甚至构成了它。

I'm not hoping on any account to escape from feeble-
mindedness,which I indicate as being present from the start. Just
like anyone else, I only escape from it as far as my means allow.
That is to say, like sur-place, like running on the spot, what is
sûr about this place doesn't ensure any verifiable progress
except in the long term.

我无论如何都不希望摆脱情感脆弱，我从一开始就指
出这一点。和其他人一样，我只能在力所能及的范围内逃
离。也就是说，就像sur-place 13一样，就像在现场跑步一样，
这个sûr（现在，接近）使得place (场)不能确保任何可验证
的进展，除非是长期的。

It is in a confabulatory way that I assert, in my way of
pondering it in my lightweight ponder, that real-ly, because

13 法语的意思是现场或在场，原地。



- 89 -

indeed the real does lie, the real entails the hole that subsists
within it, given that its consistence is no more than the
consistence of the entirety of the knot that it forms with the
symbolic and the imaginary.

我以一种虚构的方式断言，在我随意思考（pen-se）14
的方式中，实在–地（real-ly），因为实在实际上在撒谎，
实在并没有包含存在于其中的洞，因为它的一致性不超过
它与象征和想象形成的整个结的一致性。

This knot can be qualified as Borromean, which means that
it cannot be sliced through without dissolving the myth that it
renders of the subject as non-supposed, that is, as real. The latter
displays no variation from any other body that may be indicated
as a parlêtre, corps qui n’a de statut respectable, in the common
sense of the word, only by virtue of this knot.

这个结可以被称为波罗米结，这意味着，如果不消除
它所呈现非假设性的主体的神话（即实在）的话，它就不
能被切开。后者与任何其他可被称为言说的存在没有任何
区别，只是毫不体面的躯体（corps），是字面意思上的结。

14 拉康这里使用pen-se 可能暗示的是pense ce（思考这些……）



- 90 -


4

Joyce turns out to have targeted, in a privileged way,
through his art, the fourth term known as the sinthome. I shall
resume my disquisition next time by tackling this point.

乔伊斯以一种特殊的方式，通过他的艺术，而这第四
个术语称为“圣状”。下一次我将通过处理这一点来概述我的
研究。

You can see this fourth term figured in different ways up
here on the board, be it in the red ring on the far right of the
second diagram ofthe Borromean knot, or in the black ring, or
even in the following.

你可以看到这个第四项以不同的方式出现在图中，无
论是在波罗米结第二张图最右边的红环中，还是在黑环中
（注：应指本节第一张图，附在下方第二张图），甚至是在
下面的图中。




- 91 -


New figuration of the folded circle

折叠环的新图示



This is still the same folded circle. It's in a special position
here, namely, with two inflexions, that is to say, caught up in



- 92 -

itself at four points so to speak.

这仍然是相同的折叠环。它在这里处于一个特殊的位
置，即，有两个拐点，也就是说，在四个点处，可以说，
它自己陷入了困境15。

In the second figuration of the four-component knot, one or
the other of the circles at the far ends wedges in, at two different
points, the loop that is figured in one or the other of the folded
circles.

在四组分结的第二个图示中，远端的一个或另一个环
在两个不同的点处楔入折叠环中的一个和另一个中的回路。

 In the very first figuration, however, the black ring wedges
in the green, or the blue ring wedges in the red, at four different
points, because it is likewise essentially a matter of wedging.


15 正如这里所再现的，这个链环不是以最小投影的形式出现的：十个重叠可以
还原为九个交叉，产生L9a34（Rolfsen 标号为926）。然而，本课的一份非官方
手稿以略微不同的形式再现了这个链环：L10a115。




- 93 -

然而，在第一个图示中，黑色环在四个不同的点处楔
入绿色环，或蓝色环楔入红色环，因为这本质上也是楔入
（wedging）的问题。

It is still the case that it is through Joyce that I'm going to
be broaching this fourth term in so far as it completes the knot
of the imaginary, the symbolic, and the real.

现在仍然是这样，通过乔伊斯，我将讨论第四项，因
为它完成了想象、象征和实在的结合。

The whole problem lies here. How can an art target in an
expressly divinatory way the substantialization of the sinthome
in its consistence, but also in its ex-sistence and in its hole?

整个问题就在这里。一门艺术如何能够以明确的预见
方式，在它的一致性［想象］中，也在它的外—实存［实
在］和它的空洞［象征］中，将圣状实体化？

How is it that someone has been able to target through his
art the rendering of this fourth term - which I simply wanted to
show you today as. being essential to the Borromean knot - to



- 94 -

the point of getting as close to it as it is possible to get?

人怎么能够通过他的艺术来指向第四项的呈现——这
是我今天想向你们展示的。这个问题对于博波罗米结至关
重要——（要如何）尽可能接近它的点？

5 提问环节

After this exhausting task, because today I'm really feeling
very weary, I'm expecting from you what I got with greater ease
in the States than elsewhere, namely for someone to pose me a
question, whatever it might be, about what I've said today.

在完成了这项令人筋疲力尽的任务后，因为今天我真
的感到非常疲惫，我期待着从你们那里得到像我在美国时
也能得到轻松的东西，也就是说，希望有人向我提出一个
问题，关于我今天所说的，不管是什么。

I'm waiting for someone to speak up. Anyone.

我在等有人说出来。任何人。




- 95 -

So, what might have struck you as debatable in what I've
put forward today?

那么，在我今天提出的问题中，有什么可能会让你觉
得有争议？

Stuart Schneiderman - What led you to believe you'd get
anything out of Chomsky? That's something that would never
have occurred to me.

斯图尔特·施奈德曼：是什么让你相信你能从乔姆斯基
身上得到某些东西的？得到某些我永远想不到的东西的？

That is precisely why I was taken aback, sure. But we
always possess something of this kind of weakness, don't we,
some remnant of hope. Dealing as he does with linguistics, I
might have hoped to see in Chomsky some slither of
appreciation of what I have been showing with respect to the
symbolic, namely that it preserves some thing of the hole, even
when this hole is a false hole.

拉康：当然，这正是我大吃一惊的原因。但我们总是



- 96 -

有这样或那样的弱点，不是吗？总有一些希望的残余。正
如他在语言学方面所做的那样，我可能希望在乔姆斯基身
上看到某种程度上对我的赞赏——对我在象征方面所展现
的东西表示欣赏，即象征保留了一些空洞的东西，即使这
个空洞是一个虚假的空洞。

It's impossible, for instance, not to qualify the set formed
by the symptom and the symbolic as a false hole. However,
from another angle, the symptom subsists in so far as it is
hooked onto language, at least if we believe that we can modify
something in the symptom by means of a manipulation that is
said to be interpretative, that is, by playing on meaning.

例如，不可能不将症状和象征形成的集合定性为虚假
的空洞。然而，从另一个角度来看，只要症状与语言挂钩，
症状就会存在，至少如果我们相信，我们可以通过一种被
称为解释性的操纵来修改症状中的某些东西，也就是说，
通过玩弄意义。

That Chomsky should assimilate to the real something that
in my eyes belongs to the realm of the symptom, that is, that he
should mix up the symptom and the real, is very precisely what



- 97 -

took me aback.

乔姆斯基应该是把我眼中的实在事物同化到属于症状
领域，也就是说，他把症状和实在混为一谈，这正是让我
大吃一惊的原因。

Stuart Schneiderman - My next question might be a little
oisive . . . Oiseuse?

斯图尔特·施奈德曼：我的下一个问题可能有点偏激…？

Stuart Schneiderman - Thank you. Being American ...

斯图尔特·施奈德曼：谢谢。作为美国人...

Yes, you're American. And I thank you. I simply note that
once again I'm only being questioned by an American. Well, I
can only say how delighted I was, as it were, by the fact that in
the US I had people who showed me in one way or another that
my disquisition has not been in vain.

拉康：是的，你是美国人。我感谢你。我只是再次指



- 98 -

出，我只是被一个美国人质问。好吧，我只能说我是多么
高兴，因为事实上，在美国，有人以这样或那样的方式告
诉我，我的研究并没有白费。

Stuart Schneiderman - It strikes me as impossible that
someone could consider that Chomsky, who was educated in the
new tradition derived from mathematical logic, which he picked
up from Quine and Goodman at Harvard . . .

斯图尔特·施奈德曼：我觉得不可能有人会认为乔姆斯
基接受了数学逻辑新传统的教育，他是从哈佛的蒯因和古
德曼那里学来的。

But Quine is no fool, huh!

拉康：但蒯因不是傻瓜，哈！

Roland Chemama - Concerning the alternation between the
body and speech, as you've been speaking for an hour and a half,
and as you're seeking to have a more direct contact with
someone, I won dered whether in a more general way there
might not be an alternation between discourse and the body in



- 99 -

the life of a subject. Without language, would this hole not exist
due to the fact of a direct physical engagement with this real?
I'm speaking about love and jouissance.

罗兰·切玛：关于身体和语言之间的交替，因为你已经
讲了一个半小时了，而且你正在寻求与某人进行更直接的
接触，我想知道，在一个主体的生活中，从更一般的角度
来看，话语和身体之间是否存在交替。如果没有语言，这
个空洞是否会因为与该实体的直接物理接触而不存在？我
说的是爱和原乐。

That's precisely what's at issue. All the same, it's very hard
on this occasion not to consider the real as a thirdness. Let's say
that what I'm soliciting by way of response is along the lines of
a call to the real, not as something linked to the body, but as
something different. Far from the body, there is the possibility of
what last time I called resonance, or consonance. This
consonance can be found at the level of the real. Between the
two poles constituted by the body and language, the real is what
establishes an accord.

拉康：这正是问题所在。尽管如此，在这种情况下，



- 100 -

很难不将实在视为第三项。就是说，我以回应的方式所要
求的是对实在的呼唤，不是作为与身体相关的东西，而是
作为不同的东西。在远离身体的地方，有可能发生我上次
称之为共鸣（resonance）的事情。这种和谐可以在实在的
层面上找到。在由身体和语言构成的两极之间，实在其实
是在两极中建立一致的东西。

Mr X. - You were saying earlier that Chomsky makes
language an organ, and that you were taken aback by this. I was
wondering whether this might not be due to the fact that you
make libido an organ. I'm thinking of the myth of the lamella. I
wonder whether this might not be the angle from which the
question of the soul might be posed. Because casting aside the
idea of putting distance between language and the organ can
only be retrieved in the sense of an art if one cuts out the organ
at the level at which you put it, at the level of libido.

X 先生：你刚才说乔姆斯基让语言成为一种器官，你对
此感到震惊。我想知道这是否是因为你把性欲变成了一个
器官。我想到了薄片（lamella）的神话16。我想知道关于

16 见研讨班11。拉康将力比多看作人类主体身上的一个“无身体的虚假器官”。
这个器官是作为有性繁殖的人，在获得生命时必然经历的原生丧失之产物，这
种丧失在生命中成了一个无身体的器官，拉康将其称作“薄片”，它就是力比多。



- 101 -

灵魂问题是否可以从这个角度提出。因为抛开语言和器官
之间的距离这一概念，如果在你在力比多的水平上切除器
官，那么只有在艺术的意义上，才能恢复。

Libido, as its name indicates, can only be a participant in
the hole, just as much as the other modes by which the body and
the real present themselves. Clearly this is where I'm trying to
join up with the function of art.

拉康：力比多，顾名思义，只能是这个洞［实在］里
的一个参与者，就像身体和实在存在的其他模式一样。显
然，这就是我试图与艺术功能结合的地方。

This is implied in a way by what has been left blank as a
fourth term. When I say that art can even affect the symptom,
this is what I'm going to be trying to substantialize. You're quite
right to mention the myth known as the lamella myth. This hits
the right note, and I thank you for it. It's along this line that I
hope to continue.

这在某种程度上被作为留白的内容的第四项所暗示。
当我说艺术甚至可以影响症状的时候，我是要尝试去实体



- 102 -

化的。你提到了被称为“薄片的神话”的神话是对的。这是正
确的，我感谢你。我希望沿着这条路线继续下去。

Henri Cesbron-Lavau - When you speak about libido in the
text on the lamella, you say that what is remarkable about it is
its to-and-fro trajectory of invagination. Now, this image strikes
me today as being able to function like the image of the cord,
which is caught in a phenomenon of resonance, which undulates,
which forms an antinode that rises and falls, and which forms
nodes.

亨利·塞斯布龙·拉沃：当你在讨论薄片的文章中谈到力
比多时，你说它的显著之处在于它的来回内陷轨迹。现在，
这个图像今天给我的印象是，它能够像绳索的图像一样起
作用，它被一种共鸣现象所捕获，波动，形成一个波腹
（antinode），波腹上升和下降，并形成节点。

No, but it's not for nothing that in a cord the metaphor
comes from what forms a knot. What I'm trying to do is to find
what this metaphor refers to. If there are nodes and antinodes in
a vibrating cord, then it's in so far as the knot is being referenced.
I mean that language is being used in a way that goes much



- 103 -

further than what is effectively being said. People are always
limiting the scope of metaphor as such. That is to say, people
limit it to a metonymy.

拉康：不，但这并不是毫无意义的，在一根绳子里，
隐喻来自于形成一个结的东西。我想做的是找出这个隐喻
所指的东西。如果振动弦中有节点和波腹，那么它就在这
个节点所指的范围内。我的意思是，语言的使用方式远远
超过了实际上所表达的方式。人们总是限制隐喻的范围。
也就是说，人们把它局限于转喻。

Henri Cesbron Lavau - When you go from the three-
component Borromean knot to the four-component knot, into
which the symptom is introduced, the three-component
Borromean knot vanishes as such.

亨利·塞斯布龙·拉沃：当你从三组分的波罗米结转到引
入症状的四组分结时，三组分的波罗米结也随之消失。

That's quite right. It's no longer a knot. It's only held
together by the symptom.




- 104 -

拉康：没错。它不再是一个结。只有症状才把它联系
在一起。

Henri Cesbron-Lavau - From this perspective, the hope for
any cure in analysis seems to pose a problem.

亨利·塞斯布龙·拉沃:从这个角度来看，在分析中寻求任
何治疗的希望似乎带来了一个问题。

There can be no radical reduction of the fourth term, not
even in analysis, because Freud stated - we don't know by which
path- that there is an Urverdriingung, a repression that can never
be undone. It is in the very nature of the symbolic to include this
hole. This hole is what I'm aiming at, and in it I recognize the
Urverdrangung itself.

拉康：第四项不可能有根本性的减少，即使在分析中
也不可能，因为弗洛伊德指出——我们不知道通过哪条路
径——存在着一种永远无法消除的压抑。它是象征的本质，
并将这个空洞也变为自己的一部分。这个洞正是我的目标
所在，在里面我认识到了原初压抑本身。




- 105 -

Henri Cesbron-Lavau - You 've said that the Borromean
knot can't constitute a model. Could you be more explicit?

亨利·塞斯布龙·拉沃:你已经说过波罗米结不能构成一个
模型。你能说得更清楚些吗？

The Borromean knot does not constitute a model in so far
as it possesses something that imagination fails to grasp when it
gets close to it. I mean that, as such, it resists the imagining of
the knot. The mathematical approach to the knot in topology is
insufficient.

拉康：波罗米结并不构成一个模型，因为它拥有想象
在接近它时无法抓住的东西。我的意思是，正因为如此，
它抵制了对结的想象。仅用数学的方法来解释拓扑学中的
结是不够的。

I can nevertheless tell you about my experiences over the
holidays.

不过，我可以告诉你我假期的经历。




- 106 -


Knot of three crossings known as the trefoil knot

这个有着三个交叉点的结被称为三叶结

This here constitutes a knot, but not a knot between two
elements, for there is only one. This is the most straight[orward
form of knot there is, which you can make with any old piece of
string. It is the same knot as the overhand knot, though it doesn't
look the same.

这在这里构成了一个结，但不是两个元素之间的结，
因为只有一个元素。17这是最直的绳结，你可以用任何一根
旧的绳子打。它和上手绳结一样，虽然看起来不一样。

17 拉康所说的nœud à trois（指结的交叉数：3）在这里和以后都被翻译成“三
叶结”（Rolfsen 标号31）。



- 107 -


I persevered in pondering this, this discovery I made, let's
say, namely, that with this knot such as it is shown here, it is
easy to demonstrate that a Borromean knot ex-sists.

我坚持不懈地思考这个，我的这个发现，比如说，有
了这个结，就像这里所示，很容易证明波罗米结外—实存
着。

You just have to think that you could place the same knot
on an underlying surface, which is the duplicate surface without
which we wouldn't be able to set down anything at all in written
form with respect to the knots.

你只需要想一想，你可以把相同的结放在下面的表面
上，这是一个重复的表面，没有它，我们就无法以书面形
式记录任何关于结的东西。

 It's very easy to bring about the following, by setting it
down in writing, I mean - when you slip a homologous knot
under, and then, at each new step, over the knot that I'm calling
the underlying knot, you get with the greatest of ease a



- 108 -

Borromean knot. It's easy to imagine this, although you don't
come to imagine it straightaway. I had to discover it first.

我的意思是，通过把它写下来，很容易做到以下几点
——当你把一个同源的结放在下面，然后，在每一个新的
步骤上，在我称之为底层结的结上，你会非常容易地得到
一个波罗米结。这很容易想象，尽管你不能马上想象出来。
我必须先发现它。

With this trefoil knot, is there now any possibility of
bringing about a four-component Borromean knot? I spent
nearly two months racking my brains over this object. It really
does have to be called an object.

有了这个三叶结，现在有没有可能带来一个四组分波
罗米结？我花了将近两个月的时间绞尽脑汁研究这个东西。
它确实必须被称为对象。

I didn't manage to demonstrate that there ex-sists a way of
knot ting together four trefoil knots in a Borromean fashion.
Well, that doesn't prove anything. That doesn't prove that it
doesn't ex-sist.



- 109 -


我没能证明有一种方法可以将四个三叶结以波罗米式
结在一起。好吧，这不能证明什么。这并不能证明它不存
在。



Even yesterday evening, I was thinking only of managing
to demonstrate for you that it ex-sists. The worst thing was that I
didn't find the demonstrative reason for its non ex-sistence. I
simply failed.

即使在昨天晚上，我也只想设法向你证明它是外—存
在的。最糟糕的是，我没有找到它非外—实存的具体原因。
我只是失败了。

The fact that I'm unable to show that the knot of four trefoil
knots ex-sists, as a Borromean knot, doesn't prove anything.



- 110 -


事实上，我无法证明由四个三叶结组成的结，作为一
个波罗米结，并不能证明什么。

I would have to demonstrate that it cannot ex-sist, whereby
in that very impossibility, a real would have been secured. It
would be a real constituted by the fact that there is no
Borromean knot that is constituted of four trefoil knots. To
demonstrate this would be to reach a real.

我必须证明这是不可能的，因此，在这种不可能的情
况下，一个实在就会得到保障。这将是一个由以下事实构
成的实在：不存在由四个三叶结组成的波罗米结。要证明
这一点，就要到达一个实在。

To tell you what I think about it, still in my way of saying
that this is my ponder, I believe that this knot ex-sists. I mean
that this is not the path by which we would stumble upon a real.

告诉你我对它的看法，我仍然认为这是我的思考，我
相信这个结是存在的。我的意思是，这不是一条我们会遭
遇实在的道路。



- 111 -


So, I do not despair of finding it, but it's a fact that I'm
unable to show you anything of the like. Here the relationship
between showing and demonstrating is sharply divided. If and
when it were to be demonstrated, it would be easy to show it to
you.

所以，我对找到它并不绝望，但事实上，我无法向你
展示任何类似的东西。在这里，展示和证明之间的关系被
严重分割。如果且当它能被证明，那么就很容易向你展示。

Mme Y.- You said earlier that in Chomsky’s perspective
language can be an organ, and you spoke about the hand. Why
did you use this word hand? Is there, in this word, a reference to
an object that is not yet technological in the Cartesian sense of
the term? That is to say, a technology that is unaware of
language? Is the hand there to show the necessity of another
theory of technology besides Chomsky's theory.

Y 夫人：你之前说过，在乔姆斯基的观点中，语言可以
是一个器官，而你谈到了手。你为什么用手这个词？在这
个词中，有没有提到一个在笛卡尔意义上还不是技术的物



- 112 -

体？也就是说，一种不懂语言的技术？这只手是为了说明
除了乔姆斯基的理论之外，另一种技术理论的必要性。

Yes, in spite of the existence of handshakes, the hand in the
act of handshaking knows nothing of the other hand. That's what
I claim.

拉康：是的，尽管存在握手这种行为，但握手时的手
对另一只手是一无所知的。这就是我的主张。

1975 年12 月9 日





- 113 -

III
ON THE KNOT AS THE SUBJECT'S SUPPORT
论作为主体的支撑的结
Soury and Thomé
Knot and knot
Personality and paranoia
The sinthome and the unconscious
Meaning and jouissance

苏里与托米
结和结
个性与偏执
圣状与无意识
意义和原乐

If analysis was taken as seriously as I take the preparation
of my seminar, it would be so much the better, and would surely
have better results. For this, one would need in analysis the
sentiment (in the sense I spoke of the other day) of an absolute
risk.




- 114 -

如果像我准备研讨会一样认真对待精神分析，那就更
好了，而且肯定会有更好的结果。为此，人们需要对绝对
风险（absolute risk）的感觉（在我前几天所说的意义上）
进行分析。

1


The trefoil knot (circular form)

三叶结（环形）



- 115 -


I told you the other day that I had made a discovery
concerning triple knots, which I draw like this and which can be
obtained from the borromean knot - that they can be made into a
triple borromean knot, and that I struggled for two months to try
to produce a knot which would tie them together à quatre.I also
told you that the fact that I couldn't do it didn't prove anything,
apart from my clumsiness - and that I was sure it must exist.

前几天我告诉你，我从三重结中有了个发现，我是这
样画的，可以从波罗米结中得到——它们可以做成三重波
罗米结，我花了两个月时间试着打一个结，把他们一起绑
成四个。我还告诉过你，因为我的笨拙而做不到这一事实
并不能证明什么——我确信它一定存在。

That same evening, to my delighted surprise, there



- 116 -

appeared on my doorstep the man called Thomé, who came to
bring me the fruits of his collaboration with Soury: the proof
that a borromean knot consisting of four triple knots does indeed
exist - which certainly justifies my obstinacy, but makes my
incapacity no less deplorable.
Nevertheless, I welcomed the news with feelings untainted
by regrets for my impotence. My feelings were of pure and
simple enthusiasm, and I think I was able to show them some of
this when I saw them a few evenings later. They haven't been
able to give me an account of how they came to find it.

那天晚上，令我惊喜的是，一个名叫托米（Thomé）的
人出现在我家门口，他来给我带来了他与苏里（Soury）合
作的成果：他们证明确实存在由四个三重结组成的波罗米
结——这当然证明了我所坚持的是正确的，但也使我感到
自己的无能。
尽管如此，我还是抱着积极的心态欢迎这个消息。我
感受到了他们纯粹而简单的热情，我本想当我在几个晚上
之后看到他们时，我能够向他们展示其中的一些发现。他
们还没有向我说明他们是如何找到它的。

On the blackboard I have reproduced their discovery;



- 117 -

textually: my drawing takes a slightly different route, so that you
can see perhaps a little better how it's done. I would like to
commemorate this little event - which,besides. I consider not so
little - and I’m going to tell you the reasons for my research.
What is the support of this research? Not what Sarah
Kofman speaks of in her remarkable article Vautour Rouge
("'red vulture"), in which she refers to the elixirs du diable Freud
celebrates. But I recommend that you read the collection entitled
Mimesis which contains this article, which I think is worth
reading. For my part, I've only read the first, third and fifth
articles; due to the preparation of this seminar, I've had other
things to worry about. The first article - about Wittgenstein and,
so to speak, the noise made by his teaching - is the only one, to
tell the truth, that I've read right through.


我在黑板上写下了他们的发现；文字上：我的绘图采
用了稍微不同的路线，因此你们可能会更好地了解它是如
何完成的。我想纪念这个小事件——除此之外，我考虑的
并不多——我将告诉你我研究的原因。
这项研究的支撑是什么？并不是莎拉·科夫曼（Sarah
Kofman）在她非凡的文章Vautour Rouge（“红秃鹰”）中所



- 118 -

说的，她在其中提到了弗洛依德庆祝的灵丹妙药（elixirs du
diable Freud celebrates）。但我建议你阅读包含这篇文章的名
为《模仿》（Mimesis）的合集，我认为值得一读。就我而言，
我只读过第一、三、五篇文章；由于这次研讨会的准备工
作，我还有其他事情要担心。第一篇文章——关于维特根
斯坦，可以说，像他的教学所产生的嘈杂——说实话，是
唯一一篇我读完了的。

I should say that the geometry of knots - a specific, original
geometry - exorcises the so-called 'uncanny', which is
unquestionably bound up with the imaginary. But that there
should be something which allows it to be exorcised is certainly
in itself strange...

我应该说这种结的几何学——一种特定的、原始的几
何学—— 驱除了所谓的“ 离奇” （uncanny ，inquiétante
étrangeté），这无疑是与想象联系在一起的。但是应该有什
么东西可以让它被驱除，这件事本身就很奇怪……

Where should I place what is at issue here? To specify it for
you on the diagram that I gave you last year, it is round about
here.



- 119 -


The Borromean link of four trefoil knots

四个三叶结的波罗米链环



- 120 -

Here, the imaginary opens out across two circles, which
can also be noted as a drawing. I would say that a drawing
doesn't note anything, in so far as the planar diagram remains
enigmatic. Nevertheless, I'm indicating here, at the join with the
imaginary of the body, something along the lines of a specific
inhibition that would be typified by the uncanny in particular.
This is where I will allow myself to note, at least provisionally,
the place of the said uncanny.

我应该把问题放在哪里？为了在我去年给你的图表上
为你指定它，它就在这里。
在这里，想象向另外两个圆交织在一起，也可以看作
是一幅图。我想说的是，只要这幅图仍然是谜一般的，一
张图就什么也没标示出来。然而，我在这里指出，在与身
体的想象的连接处，有某种特定的禁止（压抑）（inhibition），
这种禁止（压抑）以“离奇”（uncanny，inquiétante étrangeté）
18为代表。在这里，我将标记出，至少是暂时的，我所说的
“离奇”的地方。


18 法语的字面意识是不安的奇怪。（译注）



- 121 -


RSI Diagram

RSI 示意图

The resistance experienced by the imagination to thinking
anything concerning this new geometry, is something which
strikes me because I have experienced it. It is certainly not pure
chance that Soury and Thome were fascinated by the
conjunction of imaginary, symbolic and real which was
emerging in my teaching, that they were quite especially
