Likewise, it is entirely possible to evidence the second
object on the basis of what lay behind the principle of my
deception with respect to the colouring. By inverting the colours,
I mean, and not the orientation.

我说过，只要有一个圆环（这里是红色圆环）赋予定
向就足够（用来区分两种波罗米链环）了。只要你把这两
条直线伸展到无穷远，这就变得显而易见了。在什么基础
上我们能给这些直线赋予定向呢？
类似的，完全有可能基于我对色彩的错觉的原则上，



- 344 -

来证明第二个对象（第二种链环）。这里我的意思是要通过
反转颜色而不是定向。

By inverting the colours from the first diagram, the green
and the blue, we obtain an object that is unquestionably different,
provided we leave the oriented element with the same
orientation. Indeed, why would I change the ring's orientation?
It has no reason to be changed if I have changed the pair of
colours. How would I ever recognize the non-identity of the
complete object if I were to change the orientation?


通过把（VIII-12）中的第一张图的蓝色和绿色反转，
我们得到了一个毫无疑问并不一样的对象（前提是我们不
改变被定向的对象的定向）。确实，我为什么一定要改变
（红色）圆环的定向呢？如果我把蓝色和绿色这对交换的
话就没有理由改变这个定向了。如果我要改变定向的话我
该怎么意识到这二者的不同呢？





- 345 -


Inverting the colours (blue and green)

翻转颜色（蓝色和绿色）(VIII-13)

Even if you invert this second object you will see that it's
well and truly different from the second one from earlier, for
now it's a matter of comparing the levorotary ones with each
other.
In sum, it is the orientation that has been maintained that
differentiates this triplicate from one where the triplicate could
be said to have the same presentation.

甚至如果你翻转（VIII-12）的第二个对象（的直线颜
色）的话你能看见第二个对象也变得和原来不一样了，因
为现在你只要比较这两个左旋的链环就好了。
总之，正是被保持的定向区分出了两个三元组是否是



- 346 -

相同的表征。


Comparison of the levorotary rings

圆环左旋的两种链环的比较(VIII-14)

3

The difference between the oriented ring and the coloured
pair enables us to underscore the difference between what earlier
I called the real as marked by a fallace and what is involved in
truth.
Only that which has a thrust to it is true.
What is the real's relationship with the true? The truth
about the real if I may put it like that, is that the real, the real of
this coloured pair here, has no sens, no thrust to it.



- 347 -


定向圆环和上色的一对直线的区别让我们能够强调出
我先前所说的以fallace 为标志的实在（real/réel），和涉及真
实（truth/vrai）的东西的区别。
只有有意义（sens）的东西才是真实的。
实在和真实的关系是什么？关于实在的真实/真相
（truth），如果我能这么说的话，就是实在，这对染色的直
线的实在中，是没有意义（sens）的。

This statement plays on the equivoque of the word sens.
What is the relationship between sens and what has been set
down here in written form as an orientation? One can ask the
question, and one can suggest an answer, namely - time.
The important thing is that on this occasion we are bringing
in a pair that is said to be coloured, and that this pair has no
thrust to it. Is the appearance of colour the appearance of vision,
in the sense that I have singled it out, or the appearance of the
gaze? Is it the gaze or vision that distinguishes colour? This is a
question that I shall leave in suspense for today.

这里的陈述玩了一个关于单词sens 的双关。52意为“意

52 这里的双关涉及法语sens 的两个含义：一个和英语的sense 一样，有意义、



- 348 -

义”的sens 和这里所写的意为定向的sens 有什么区别？会
有人问这个问题，而有人可能会回答：区别在于时间。
重要的是，在这一情况下我们正引入一对染色（的直
线/东西），而这一对直线中是没有sens（意义）的。在我所
做的区分之下，颜色的表象是视觉（vision）的表象还是凝
视（regard）的表象？区分颜色的视觉还是凝视？这是我今
天将搁置的一个问题。

This notion of the coloured pair is designed to suggest that
in sex there is nothing more than what I shall call the Being of
colour, which in itself suggests that there can be woman with
man's colour or man with woman's colour.
If we sustain what is involved in the symbolic by means of
the / red ring, then the sexes on this occasion lie in opposition as
the imaginary and the real, as the idea and the impossible, to use
my terms.

我设计这对涂色的直线的概念，是为了表明在性别中
仅有的是我称作是颜色之存有（Being/l'être），这一存在自
身暗示着可能存在“男性颜色”的女性或者“女性颜色”

意思之意，比如sens propre（本义）；另一个则是方向、定向的意思，比如en
sens inverse（相反方向）。



- 349 -

的男性。
如果我们通过红色的圆环来维持象征所涉及的东西的
话，那么这一情况下的两个性别作为想象和实在——或者
用我的术语，作为理念和不可能性——相对而立。

Is it altogether sure however, that the real is always what is
in question? I asserted that in the case of Joyce it is rather the
idea and the sinthome, as I've been calling it. Hence the
clarification that results from this regarding what a woman is.
Here, she is not-all, on account of not being grasped, on account
of remaining foreign to Joyce, on account of having no thrust to
her, no meaning for him. Besides, does a woman ever carry a
meaning for man?

然而，我们是否就可以完全确信实在总是那有争议的
东西？我断言在詹姆斯·乔伊斯（Joyce）的案例中，（实在）
更像是我所说的理念（the idea）和圣状（the sinthome）。因
此，我们通过考虑女人是什么得到了（对这一结论的）澄
清。这里，她（女人）是非全的（女人不是整全的）（not-
all/pas-toute），因为她没有被捕获到、因为她对乔伊斯来说
总是尤其陌生的、因为她对他而言没有意义（sens）。此外，
有任何一个女人曾给男人带来过意义吗？



- 350 -


Man is the bearer of the idea of the signifier. This idea is
essentially sustained in lalingua by syntax. It is no less the case
that if there is something that may be supposed in history, then it
is that what I have called lalingua is begotten by the set of
women, faced with a tongue that can be broken down, Latin on
this occasion, because this is what is at issue at the origin of our
tongues.

男人是能指的观念的承受者。而能指的观念本质上是
由句法中生成的“牙牙语”（lalangue）所支撑的。尽管如此，
如果说历史可以做假设的话，那可能是女人这一集合在一
种衰变的语言面前——（对我们来说）可能是拉丁语——
孕育了我所说的牙牙语，因为这就是关于我们语言的起源
的争执。

What is at issue is this fact of saying, examined with
respect to what is involved of lalingua, with respect to what may
have guided one out of the two sexes towards what I shall call
the prosthesis of the equivoque, because what typifies lalingua
among all the others is the equivoques that are possible in it, as I
have illustrated with the equivoque between deux and d' eux.



- 351 -

In each case, lalingua was begotten by a set of women.

在对牙牙语所涉及的内容做过研究后，有人可能会问，
是什么引导两性之一走向我称之为字首弥音的双关。因为
牙牙语相对其它语言的特征在于，牙牙语可能有deux（它
两）和d’eux（他俩）的双关。
各种情况下，牙牙语总是由女人这一集合所孕育的。

I want to indicate something in relation to this point. We've
spoken about a good many things today, except for what it is
that constitutes the specificity of the Borromean link. The
Borromean link would have no cause to be were it not for what
I'm drawing here. And which, as usual, I'm drawing badly. The
specificity of the Borromean link is indeed this, which I have
already presented.

关于这点我想说明一些事情。我们今天已经谈论了很
多东西了，除了没谈波罗米链环究竟有什么专属特性。假
若没有我现在所画的（这类结构）的话，波罗米链环就是
无关紧要的；然后，我画的一如既往的难看。波罗米链环
的特征就正是我所展现的这个东西，这个伪洞。53

53 译注：看上去中间有个洞但其实两个圆圈很容易就分离了。



- 352 -



false hole/faux-trou

伪洞(VIII-15)

As I underlined earlier, in a circle there is a hole. The fact
that one is able, by taking one circle and joining to it another, to
form this hole that consists in what passes through the midst of
it, and which is neither the hole of one nor the hole of the other,
this is what I am calling the false hole.
Should something - a straight line or a circle - traverse this
false hole, then the latter thereby comes to be as it were, verified.
The essence of the Borromean link hinges on this verifying of
the false hole, on the fact that this verifying transforms it,
making it real.




- 353 -

正如我先前强调的，圆周的当中有一个洞。我称为伪
洞的，就是（像上面这样）两个圆周连接起来形成一个包
含通过其正中东西的洞、而这个洞并不是两个圆周本身的
洞（的这一结构）。
如果此时，有什么东西——一条直线或者一个圆周—
—穿过了这一伪洞，那么伪洞就因此被实现/证实了（be
verified ）。波罗米链环的本质就倚赖于对伪洞的证实
（verifying），因为正是这一证实改变了它、让它变为实在
的。


Transformation of the false hole, making it real

伪洞变为实在的(VIII-16)

Now, having had an opportunity to reread my Signification



- 354 -

of the phallus, I had the pleasant surprise of finding in the very
first line a mention of the knot. That was at a time when I was
still a long way from turning my attention to the Borromean
knot. On that occasion, the knot was the mainspring of
castration.
It is indeed the phallus that has the role of verifying the
false hole, making it real.
It is in so far as the sinthome forms a false hole with the
symbolic that there can be any praxis whatsoever that is to say,
something that emanates from the dire, from the fact of saying,
from what on this occasion I shall call art-dire to slide towards
ardour.

先前我重新读了一下我的《菲勒斯的意指》，然后我在
其中提到扭结的第一行得到了惊喜的发现。那时我还远没
有把注意力转向波罗米结。那里，扭结是阉割的主要动因。
菲勒斯确实就是证实伪洞、使其变为实在的角色。
就圣状用象征形成一个伪洞而言，确实可以有某种实
践，也即某种从话语（dire）中产生的东西，从我在这里所
称的艺术话语（l’art-dire）滑向激情（l’ardeur）。54


54 译注：这里又是语言游戏。l’art-dire 和l’ardeur 发音很相近。



- 355 -

Joyce didn't know that he was fashioning the sinthome, I
mean, that he was simulating it. He was oblivious to it and it is
by dint of this fact that he is a pure artificer, a man of savoir-
faire, which is what is likewise known as an artist.
The only real that verifies anything whatsoever is the
phallus, in so far as it is the support of the function of the
signifier. In that article, I underline that this function creates all
that is signified.
 It is still the case - I shall add in order to take this up next
time - that there oughtn't to be any but this to verify this real.

乔伊斯并不知道当时他在创造圣状，我是说，他在模
拟圣状。他对此毫无察觉，因为它是一个纯粹的工匠、一
个专有技术的男人，此外可能被叫做艺术家。
菲勒斯是唯一能够证实任何东西的实在，就其支撑着
能指功能而言如此。在那篇文章中，我强调了这一功能创
造了所有被（能指）指向的东西。很有必要说明——我补
充一下，这样我下次就好讲了——除此之外没有别的东西
能证实这一实在。

1976 年3 月9 日





- 356 -

VIII
ON SENS, SEX, AND THE REAL
关于意义、性和实在
Orientation of the real, foreclosure of sens
The real is tied to nothing
Joyce's sinthomé is unanalysable
The phallic function, between
fantasy and phonation Folisophy

实在的方向，意义的除权
实在与无相关联
乔伊斯的圣状是无法被分析的
菲勒斯的功能，在幻想和发声的哲学之间

This is the most recent thing that Soury and Thomé have
given me. It's a Borromean knot of my sort, made of two infinite
straight lines and something circular. You can see, doubtless
with a bit of effort, that it's Borromean.

这是苏里和托米最近给我的一个东西。这是我的一种
波罗米结，由两个无限延长的直线和一个环状的东西组成。



- 357 -

你可以轻易看出，这是一种波罗米结。


Borromean link by Soury and Thomé

苏里和托米给出的波罗米链

The only excuse I have for saying something to you today -
because, in truth, I need some excuse, at least in my eyes - is
that this is going to be sense, sensible. In view of which I shall
not be doing what I would like to, which would be to give you
un bout de reel, some odd or end of the real. It cannot go by any
other name. You will see that I'm going to be clarifying this. I
am thereby reduced to telling myself that there is something
sensible that can serve a purpose, provisionally. This provisional
is, however, fragile. I'm not sure how long it will be able to



- 358 -

serve its purpose.

我今天在这里给你们讲课的唯一的理由——事实上，
至少在我眼中，我需要这样一个理由——就是这将会是有
意义的。在这个角度上，我将无法做到我想要的。我想给
你们一些“实在”，或者是实在的一小部分（un bout de
Réel）。我不知道该如何用其他的名字称呼它，我之后会讲
明白的。我开始认为有意义的东西可以暂时起效。但这暂
时的效果是脆弱的。我的意思是我不知道他会起效多长时
间。

1

I've been much preoccupied with Joyce of late, and I'm
going to tell you why Joyce is so stimulating. Joyce is
stimulating because he suggests, though it's merely a suggestion,
an easy way of presenting him. In view of which, and here
indeed lies his worth and his weight, everybody has been hitting
the wall with this, even my friend Jacques Aubert who is here in
the front row and before whom I feel unworthy.

这几天，我一直沉浸在乔伊斯（的作品里）。乔伊斯很



- 359 -

有启发性，这是因为乔伊斯自己暗示了一种简单的呈现他
的方式；在这个角度上，这当然是他的重要性所在。所有
人都在这里跌了跟头——哪怕是我的朋友雅克·奥伯特，
他在这方面是最杰出的人之一，在他面前我也略逊一筹。

Jacques Aubert doesn't manage any more than anyone else,
no more than the said Adams who has pulled off some tours de
force in this area, to reach this easy way of presenting him.
Perhaps in a while I will tell you, rather than suggesting to you,
why this is so.

雅克·奥伯特不能——至少不比任何其他人更能，不
比那个在这个流派创作了许多杰作的那个亚当斯更能——
尝试用一个简单的方式来呈现乔伊斯。我大概将会很快直
接告诉你们，而不是暗示你们，为什么会是这样。

Of course, I too have been dreaming of this easy way of
presenting Joyce. This is to be taken in the literal sense, because
I dreamt about it just last night. Evidently - evidement, as one
says, emptying out - you were my audience, but I was not an
actor, and even, I was not an actor in the least. I was sharing
with you the way that I - not an actor in the least, but instead



- 360 -

what I would call a mediocre penman - judge characters who
were not mine. Evidently, in this respect, I was overstepping my
role, or rather, I had no part. It was something of the
psychodrama kind. This is an interpretation.

同样的，我也梦想过这样一个简单的呈现他的方式。
我昨晚就在想这件事。显然，大家所说的显然evidement，
掏心掏肺地说，你是我的观众，但我不是演员。我告诉你
的这个方式是（与其说是作为演员，我会说它是个庸常的
文员（scribouillard））我评判的角色不是我自己的，我显然
是在超越自己的角色，或者更确切地说，我没有角色。这
有点像心理剧，一种心理解读。

That Joyce should make me dream of functioning in this
way must have a value, a value that is, moreover, not easy to
extract, because as I said he suggests to anyone and everyone
that there must be a Joyce that is easy to handle. He suggests
this due to the fact that there is such a thing as psychoanalysis,
and a whole load of people go scurrying off down this trail. It is
not, however, because I am a psychoanalyst and, by the same
stroke, overly concerned, that I should for bid myself from
picturing him in this light, for there is all the same something



- 361 -

objective here.

乔伊斯让我想这种呈现的运作方式一定有其价值，这
种价值难以从别处提取，因为正如我所说，它向所有人暗
示，一定有一个可轻易解读的乔伊斯。这一暗示是基于精
神分析的存在，而且很多人都沿着这条理路。但这并不是
因为我是一个精神分析师，同样的道理，我过于担心，我
应该让自己不要用这种观点来描绘他，因为这里同样有一
些客观的东西。

I would say that Joyce is an a-Freud, playing on the word
affreux. Thus, he is an a-Joyce. Every object, except the object
that I call a, which is an absolute, abides by a relation. The
annoying thing is that there is language, and that relations are
expressed within it by means of épithète, relatives. Relatives
push towards yea or nay.

我想说，乔伊斯是一个“a–弗洛伊德”，按affreux（a-
Freud）（可怕的）来说55，他是一个“a–乔伊斯”。所有的
客体，除了那个我称为客体小a 的绝对者，都与一个关系连

55 三语双关：affreux 在法语中的意思是“可怕的”，“很糟的”；弗洛伊德的名
字是乔伊斯（弗洛伊德，joy）在德语中的粗略“翻译”；前缀a-似乎意味着否
定（如乔伊斯名字的一个版本“詹姆斯·乔伊利斯（James joyless）”）。



- 362 -

接着。恼人的是，存在语言，而关系是在语言中被表述的
——用定语（épithète）的形式表述。正是定语（修饰语）
导向了（push towards）“是”与“否”（« oui ou non »）。

Upon this, a certain Charles Sanders Peirce built his own
brand of logic, which due to the emphasis that he lays upon
relation leads him to construct a ternary logic. This is precisely
the same path that I have been following, with the slight
difference that I call the things at issue by their name - symbolic,
imaginary, and real, in the correct order. To push towards yea or
nay is to push towards the couple. This is so because there is a
rapport, a relation, between language and sex. This relation has
certainly not yet been altogether clarified, but I have, as it were,
made a dent in it.

查尔斯·桑德斯·皮尔斯在此之上建立了他的逻辑，
这是因为他对于关系的强调，使他建立了一个三元逻辑。
这和我的路径完全一致，我只不过是以他们原来的名字来
命名——象征，想象，实在，用正确的顺序排列。导向"是"
或"不是"就是导向一对（伴侣）。这是因为语言和性之间存
在着一种关系。这种关系当然还没有完全明确，但可以说
我已经开了个头。



- 363 -


Did you catch that? In saying I've made a dent, I realize
that I'm forming a metaphor. And what does this metaphor mean?
I can speak about metaphor in the general sense, but I'll leave it
up to you to find out what this one means.

你看，通过使用"开头"这个词，我意识到我在做一个隐
喻。那么这个隐喻是什么意思呢？我可以在一般意义上谈
论这个隐喻。但这意味着什么呢？我将留给你们自己去发
现。

Metaphor indicates this and nothing else - sexual relation.
With the proviso that metaphor proves de facto, due to the fact
that it exists, that sexual relation amounts to taking a bladder for
a lantern , that is to say, the best one can utter to express a mix-
up. A bladder can become a lantern on the condition you put a
flame inside, but so long as there is no flame, it's not a lantern.

隐喻除了表明了性关系之外再没有任何意思。除了它
证明事实上——因为它（隐喻）存在——性关系就是把膀
胱错当成灯笼。这是表达困惑的最好方式——如果里面有
火，膀胱就可以成为灯笼，但只要里面没有火，它就不是



- 364 -

一个灯笼。56

Where does this flame come from? The flame is the real.
The real sets fire to everything. But it's a cold fire. The fire that
burns is a mask, if I may say so, of the real. The real is to be
sought on the other side, on the side of absolute zero. They've
got to it, even that. There is no limit to the high temperatures
one may imagine. For the time being there is no imaginable
limit. The only thing that partakes of the real is the lower limit.
This is what I call something orientable. This is why the real is
orientable.

火从哪里来？火是实在的。实在把一切都点燃了。但
这是一团冰冷的火。燃烧的火焰是实在的面具。而实在要
在另一边找到，在绝对零度的一边。我们毕竟已经到了这
一步。我们可以想象的高温是没有限制的，同样，没有可
以被想象把握的限制。唯一属于实在之物的是底层限制。
这就是我称之为可定向的东西。这就是为什么实在是可定
向的。


56 …拿一个膀胱当灯笼（prendre une vessie pour une lanterne）是法语中对错误
的谚语表达，在英语中类似于“认为月亮是由绿奶酪制成的”。



- 365 -

There is an orientation, but this orientation is not the thrust
of it. What does that mean? I'm going back on what I said last
time when I suggested that the sens, the thrust of something,
might be its orientation. But orientation is not sens because it
excludes the bare fact of the copulation of the symbolic and the
imaginary, which is where the thrust of it lies. The orientation of
the real, on my territory, forecloses sens.

存在一个方向（orientation），但这个方向不是一个意义。
这是什么意义？这意味着我，与我上次说的一样，暗示了
意义可能是方向，但是方向不是一个意义，因为它并不带
有意义所带有的：象征和想象的耦合。实在的方向，在我
的公式中，排斥（forecloses，forclot）了意义。

I'm saying this because yesterday evening I was asked
whether there are other types of foreclosure besides the one that
results from the foreclosure of the Name-of-the-Father. It's quite
certain that foreclosure has something more radical about it. In
the end, the Name-of-the-Father is something slight. Yet it's
certain that this is where it can serve a purpose, in the stead of
the foreclosure of sens by the orientation of the real. Well, we
haven't got there yet.



- 366 -


我这样说是因为昨晚有人问我是否有除了因父之名而
产生的排斥之外的排斥。当然，排斥有着更激进的位面，
因为父之名说到底是很轻的。但它当然可以在这里起效，
以代替实在的方向对于意义的排斥，不过，我们还没有说
到那里。

One has to break oneself up, if I may say so, with a new
imaginary that establishes sens. This is what I'm trying to
establish with my language, which has the advantage of
wagering on psychoanalysis inasmuch as I try to institute it as
discourse, that is to say, as semblance at its most vraisemblable,
its most plausible. All in all, psychoanalysis is nothing more
than a short-circuit via meaning, via sens as such. Just now I
defined this as the copulation of language - since it is by means
of this that I support the unconscious - with our body.

我们必须打破并进入一种新的，与意义相关联的想象
图景。这就是我试图用我的语言来建立的东西，它的优势
在于能让我对精神分析下注，因为我试图将其转变为话语
——也就是说，成为最真实（vraisemblable）的伪装。简而
言之，精神分析只不过是一个意义短路的例子——像我方



- 367 -

才定义的：语言与身体的媾和，正因此我才支持无意识。

I have to tell you that in the meantime I went to listen to
Jacques Aubert, somewhere else, where you weren't invited, and
there I offered some reflections on the Ego, what the English
call Ego, and what the Germans call the Ich. I cogitated around
this Ego on the basis of a knot that had itself been cogitated by a
mathematician who goes by the name of Milnor. He came up
with an idea of the link which I'm going to have to draw.

我得告诉你，与此同时，我去一个没有邀请你们的地
方听雅克·奥伯特的演讲，我在那里对自我进行了一些思
考，英国人称之为ego，而德国人则是Ich。我一直在用纽
结来思考自我，一个由一位叫米尔诺（Milnor）的数学家提
出的纽结。他发明了链这一概念，我得把它画出来。

I'm redoing it because, of course, I'm getting in a muddle,
as I do every time I draw a knot. This is not the first time this
has happened to me in front of you. Here it is, drawn properly.
It's a link with two components. You should be able to see that it
holds fast.




- 368 -

我当然得重新画一遍，因为我自己脑子又乱了，我每
次画结的时候都会这样。这也不是我第一次在你们面前出
岔子了。这里，这样就画好了。这是一个由两个部分组成
的结。你们应该能看出来，它是能够固定住的。


Link described by Milnor

米尔诺所描述的结57

Suppose, says Milnor, that you allow that in any such two
component link one of the components is able to cross itself in
such a way that what formerly crossed over now crosses under.
Then, there is no more link. Of course, there are an abundance

57 这种链环，在米尔诺1954 年的论文中有所描述（载于《数学年刊》Annals
of Mathematics 59 (2): 177-195），更广为人知的是怀特海德链环（以J.H.C. 怀特
海德命名）或L5a1（Rolfsen 标号521）。



- 369 -

of further examples. What I now offer to your astuteness is the
remark that if you double each of the components in the link,
this will no longer be true, as invraisemblable, as implausible, as
this may seem. You will check this, I hope. I didn't bring my
drawings so I shan't venture to show you its writhe. If a figure-
of-eight loop crosses itself it easily comes free. Why ever should
this not be true when there are two of them, two figures-of-eight
and two ovals? I'll come back to this next time. Not only is there
an obstacle, it is also radically impossible to separate the four
components.

米尔诺说，假设你允许在任意一个链中，使原本的一
个从下面穿过去的部分从上面穿过去——纽结和连接就不
再存在了。我想提醒你们这些聪明人的是：如果你将第一
个纽结上的每个要素加倍，（上面的假设）就不再成立了，
虽然这看起来不太可能（invraisemblable）——（我希望你
们能自己检查这个，我没带我的草稿，我不想冒险向你们
展示这是如何打结的）。如果一个“8”字结穿过了自身，
那么它很容易就散开了。那么为什么当有两组结的时候，
也就是有两个八字结和两个椭圆的时候，这个节就无法这
样被解开了呢？我下次再来讲这个点，不单单是说这里有
一个障碍，而是说这四个部分完全不可能被分开来。



- 370 -


2

On this point, I need to tell you that I cannot trace out all
the algorithms of the S(Ⱥ) type, S of barred A.

在这一点上，我必须说，我没法描绘出S(Ⱥ)，被划斜
杆A 的S。

What does it mean that I protest, in my Seminar Encore -
so it would seem, because of course I've never read it, indeed I'd
completely forgotten it, but a few others have apparently been
wondering what it means - against confusingly equating the S(Ⱥ)
with the phi function. Not the lowercase φ, but the capital Φ
which is a function, as is implied by what I indicated, namely -
there exists an x for whom this function is negative, ∃X ΦX
തതതതത.

我在我的研讨班Encore（再来一次）中的论点似乎
（当然，我自己从没读过我的研讨班，这是给其他人读的）
被一些人反对（我完全忘了是谁），他们认为S(Ⱥ)与菲勒斯
函数是混淆的。我说的不是小写的φ，而是大写的Φ，这个
函数中存在一个x 由此这个函数是否定的：∃X ΦX
തതതത



- 371 -


Of course, the ideal of the ma theme is that everything
should correspond. It is precisely in this respect that the
matheme wades in on the real. Indeed, this correspondence is
not the final term of the real, contrary to what people imagine,
without knowing why. As I said earlier, we can only reach odds
and ends of the real.

当然，理想的公式（mathéme）中所有的东西都是对应
的。的确，这就是我的公式（mathéme）对实在的补充。与
我们想象的相反（我不知道为什么），这种对应关系不是实
在的结束。正如我之前所说，我们只能接触到部分的实在。

This real, the real at issue in what is called my thought, is
always an odd or an end, a core. It is certainly a core around
which thought embellishes, but the mark as such of this real is
that it doesn't tie on to anything. This at least is how I conceive
of the real.

实在界，就是所谓我的思想中的那个，总是一个部分，
一个核。思想围绕着这个核心进编织着，但是这种实在的
印记本身与任何物无关。至少我是这么理解实在的。



- 372 -


There have been little points of emergence throughout
history. One day, a certain Newton found one of these odds or
ends of the real. That bloody well put the wind up all the
thinkers, foremost among them the likes of Kant who may be
said to have turned Newton into a sickness. Furthermore, all of
them, all the pondering creatures of the time, each in their own
way turned him into one. That came raining down not only on
the men but on the women too. Madame du Chatelet wrote a
whole book on the Newtonian System where she takes abundant
leave of her senses. It is all the same extraordinary that reaching
an odd or end of the real should produce such an effect. It is,
however, from here that one should start off. It is the very sign
that one has reached the core

在历史中不断地有（实在）出现的小迹象。有一天，
一个叫牛顿的人发现了一部分的实在。这吓坏了所有的思
想者，尤其是康德。他可以说是把牛顿变成了自己的一种
疾病。事实上，每个人，当时所有思想者都以自己的方式
得了这个病。这不仅影响了男人，还影响了女人。杜·夏特
莱夫人写了一整本关于牛顿体系的书，但这本书基本是胡
言乱语。这太惊人了，当接触到一部分的实在时，居然产



- 373 -

生了如此大的影响。但这应该是出发点，即一个人已经接
触到核心的标志。

I'm trying to offer you an odd or end of the real with
respect to where we are. We are at the place of this incredible
story that is the human race.

现在，我试图给你们一部分的实在，在这个皮囊中，
我们存在。我们存在与这个不可思议的历史的皮囊中，这
个历史就是人类的思想，就是人类。

I tell you in this respect that there is no sexual relation, but
this is embellishment because it partakes of the yea or nay. As
soon as I say there is no . .. ,it's already very suspicious as to
whether this truly is some odd or end of the real because the
mark of the real is that it is tied to nothing, as I said a moment
ago.

我告诉过你们，没有性关系。但这只是修饰的说法，
因为它是“是”或“否”的一部分。当我说“否”时，很
可能他已经不再是一部分的实在，因为实在的烙印
（stigmate）与任何物无关，我前面说过。



- 374 -


One recognizes oneself only in what one has. One never
recognizes oneself in what one is. This is implicit in what I have
been putting forward. It is implicit in the fact, which Freud
acknowledged, that there is an unconscious.

一个人只通过其所拥有的来认识自身。一个人从来无
法通过其所是的来认识到自身——我之前说的这句话暗示
了这一点，即存在着弗洛伊德认识到的无意识。这是精神
分析的第一步。

That one never recognizes oneself in what one is is the first
step of psychoanalysis, because what one is, when one is a man,
belongs to the realm of copulation, that is to say, to what
reroutes this said copulation into the no less uttered, and
significantly so, copula constituted by the verb to be. In its
change of direction towards the copula, language meets the
proof that it is a rerouting that is altogether vessie, bladder-like,
that is to say, obscure. Obscure is but a metaphor here, because
were we to have an odd or end of the real, we would know that
light is no darker than shade, and vice versa.




- 375 -

因为一个人是秩序的产物，当他是一个男人的时候，
是属于交配的秩序，也就是说，将所谓的交配（copulation）
重定向到由动词to be 构成的同等且重要的所谓的系词
（copula）。在由系词所致的转调中，语言被证明是一条完
全模糊的，膀胱一般的弯路。模糊性在这里只是一个隐喻，
因为如果我们有了一部分的实在，我们将会知道光明不会
比阴影更模糊，反之亦然。

The metaphor copula is not proof in and of itself. This is
the way of proceeding that the unconscious has - it only gives
traces, which not only efface themselves of their own accord,
but which any use of discourse strives to efface, the analytic
discourse like the rest. You yourselves will reckon only on
erasing the traces of mine from discourse, since I'm the one who
started out by furnishing the analytic discourse with its status,
on the basis of affecting a semblance of the object a, that is, my
naming of man putting himself in the place of the refuse that he
is - at the very least in the eyes of a psychoanalyst, who has
good reason to know this, for he himself puts himself in this
place. One has to go via this determined refuse so as, perhaps, to
retrouver, to find again something that might belong to the order
of the real.



- 376 -


系词的隐喻本身不是一个证明。它是无意识行进的方
式。它只提供了痕迹，而这些痕迹不仅会抹去自己，而且
任何话语的使用都会抹去这些痕迹，包括分析师话语。你
们自己将会想着抹除我的话语的痕迹，因为正是我开始给
予分析话语其地位：在影响对象a 的表象的基础上，或者正
如我所说，人类把自己放在垃圾的后面，人占据了污秽之
地，然而，至少在精神分析师的眼中，他有充分的理由知
道这一点，因为是他自己占据了那个地方。这种决定性的
污秽必须被穿过，这样一来也许可以在实在界中找到些什
么。

But you see, I'm using the word retrouver. This is already a
slide, as though everything of this order had already been found.
Such is the snare of history. History is the greatest of fantasies,
if it may be expressed like this. Behind the history of the facts in
which historians take an interest, there lies myth. Myth is always
captivating, the proof being that, having carefully borne witness
to the sinthome of Dublin that only has any soul to it by dint of
his own, Joyce doesn't fail - fabulously - to fall into the Vico
myth that sustains Finnegans Wake. The only thing that
nevertheless saves him from this is that Finnegans Wake



- 377 -

presents itself as a dream. Not only is it a dream, but it denotes
how Vico is a dream just as much as Madame Blavatsky's
diffuse bleed, the manvantara and everything that ensues, the
idea of a rhythm that I fell back into myself with my retrouver
just now. One never finds again - or else, this denotes how one
only ever turns round in circles - one. finds.

你看，我刚用了“找到”（retrouver）这个词，这已经
是一种滑动了，仿佛这个秩序的一切都已经找到了。这就
是历史的陷阱。历史是最伟大的幻想：在历史学家感兴趣
的事实的历史背后，有一个神话。而神话总是令人着迷的。
乔伊斯，收到他自己的启发，在自觉地见证了《都柏林人》
的圣状后，不失时机地陷入支撑着《芬尼根守灵夜》的维
柯（Vico）神话中。唯一能使它免于这种情况的是，《芬尼
根守灵夜》是以梦境的形式呈现的，而且，维科也被描绘
成一个梦。最后，布拉瓦茨基夫人（Mme Blavatsky）的胡
言乱语、曼瓦塔拉（Mahanvantara）和所有随之而来的东西
也都是梦，以及我在之前用“找到”（retrouver）一词（旧
病）复发的节奏。我们从不“再”找到（retrouver 的re 为
“再”之意），或者说我们只是在兜圈子。我们只能——找
到。




- 378 -

The only advantage of this. finding again is to highlight
what I'm indicating, that there cannot be any progress, that one
only ever goes round in circles. Even so, there is perhaps another
way of explaining that there is no progress. It is that there is no
progress but bearing the stamp of death, which Freud
underscores by triebing this death, if I can put it like that, by
making it a Trieb. This was translated into French by pulsion or
pulsion de mort. I don't know why they didn't find a better
translation when there was the word dérive. The death drive is
the real inasmuch as it can only be pondered qua impossible.
This means that each time it rears its head it is imponderable. To
approach this impossible could never constitute a hope, because
this imponderable is death, whose real grounding is that it
cannot be pondered.

这一“找到”的唯一好处是，它强调了我所指出的问
题，即没有任何进步，我们在兜圈子。但这儿还有另一个
解释，没有除了那被死亡标记的进步之外的进步，弗洛伊
德早已用对死亡的驱动（de trieber）指出了这点，并将其作
为“驱力”（Trieb），在法语中被翻译成pulsion（冲动），la
pulsion de mort（死亡冲动）（我不知道为什么）——为什么
不能是dérive？死亡驱力是实在的，因为它只能被认为是不



- 379 -

可能的——也就是说，每次它在转角处窥视，都是不可被
思考的。我们不能希望去把握那一不可能性，因为它是不
可思考的；它是死亡，而实在界的基础就是它是不能被思
考的。

What is incredible is that Joyce - who harboured the
greatest contempt for history, however futile, which he qualifies
as a nightmare whose character is to bring down upon us those
big words that he says make us so unhappy - was only able to
find the solu ... tion of writing Finnegans Wake, namely a dream,
which like any dream is a nightmare, even if it's a toned-down
nightmare. With one slight difference, says he, and this is how
Finnegans Wake is fashioned, it's that the dreamer is not any one
character, he is the dream itself. It is in this respect that Joyce
slides ... slides ... slides to Jung, slides to the collective
unconscious. There is no better proof than Joyce that the
collective unconscious is a sinthome, for it cannot be said that
Finnegans Wake, in his imagination, does not partake of this
sinthome.

令人称奇的是，乔伊斯，对历史抱有最强烈的蔑视
（虽然他的蔑视是无用的）——将历史称为梦魇，向我们



- 380 -

释放了他所强调的，给我们带来如此大伤害的巨大邪恶—
—最后只能找到一个解决之道：写出《芬尼根守灵夜》，尽
管作为一场梦写出来，就像任何一场梦都是梦魇，即使是
一次温和的梦魇。此外他说，做梦者并不是个特定的角色，
他是梦本身，这也是《芬尼根守灵夜》的创作方式。此处，
乔伊斯滑向了荣格，滑向了集体无意识。除了乔伊斯，没
什么能更好地证明集体无意识是一个圣状，因为没有人能
说，在他的想象中，《芬尼根守灵夜》不形成这个圣状的一
部分。

So, the sign of my entanglement is indeed Joyce, precisely
inasmuch as what he puts forth, and in a way that is quite
especially that of an artist because he has the know ... how to
pull it off, is the sinthome, and a sinthome such that there is
nothing to be done to analyse it.

乔伊斯确实是我纠结的标志，在他发展的独特艺术手
法中，有着圣状，而这样的东西是无法被分析的。

A rock-solid catholic, as was Joyce, who never did
anything to compromise his proper upbringing by the Jesuits, a
catholic, a genuine article - but of course, there are no genuine



- 381 -

ones here, you weren't raised by Jesuits, not a single one of you
- well, a catholic is unanalysable.

我最近说过——像乔伊斯这样死硬的天主教徒永远不
会说那些耶稣会士没有好好培养他长大，一个真正的天主
教徒（但当然，这里没有真正的天主教徒，你们这些人不
是由耶稣会士培养出来的）——一个天主教徒是无法被分
析的。

3

On that score, someone made the remark that I said the
same thing about the Japanese. It was Jacques-Alain Miller, of
course, who didn't let the opportunity slip by. Well, I stick by
this, though it's not for the same reason. Since the Jacques
Aubert evening, to which you were not invited, I've seen a film.
It too was Japanese. I was taken aback because it's female
eroticism. I wasn't expecting that in going to see a Japanese film.
Watching it, I started to understand the power of Japanese
women. It was a private screening, but I do hope that it will be
certified for general release. If you scamper around a bit you
will manage to see it on one of the restricted viewing screens.



- 382 -

I'm not going to set down a dividing line just on the basis of a
film, but female eroticism seems to be carried to its extreme
point here, and this extreme is the fantasy, no more nor less, of
killing the man. But even that does not suffice. Having killed
him, she goes further still. Afterwards - why after? there lies the
doubt - the Japanese woman in question, who is a maitresse
femme, a boss lady, if ever there was, slices off her partner's
dick. That's what it's called. One wonders why she didn't do it
beforehand. We know that it's a fantasy, all the more so given
that there's a great deal of blood in the film. I'm fine with the
idea that the corpus cavernosum might be blocked, but in the
end, what do I know? I don't know how this plays out after death.
There is a shade of what just now I called doubt. This is where
one can clearly see that castration is not the fantasy. It is not so
easy to situate it in its function in analysis because it can be
fantasmatized.

在这个问题上某人提醒过我（当然是雅克-阿兰·米勒，
他没浪费这个机会）他说我曾在讨论日本人时说过同样的
话。我保留我的观点。我这么说的原因是不同的。自从我
与雅克·奥伯特在一个晚上一起看了一部日本电影。那是
在一个小电影院，你们也没被邀请，不过我也不想给你们



- 383 -

什么坏的想法。我当时，应该说，目瞪口呆，因为那是女
性性的色情。我从未期望会在日本电影中看到这样的色情，
从那时起，我开始理解日本女性的力量。女性性的色情在
那里被发扬到极致，而这种极致，不多不少，恰恰是杀死
男人的幻想。但即使如此也不够，在他被杀死后，必须更
进一步。在此之后，为什么是在此之后？这正是我们所问
的，那个日本女人，作为她伴侣的情妇，将他的阴茎切掉
了。有人可能好奇为什么不在（杀死他）之前将其切掉。
因为这是一个幻想，仅此而已——我不知道在他死后这是
如何发生的，但电影中充满了血腥，我宁愿它的勃起组织
能保持坚挺，但毕竟我不知道它死后是什么样的。我刚说
的一些话是值得疑问的。在这儿，谁都能看出阉割不是一
个幻想。将这一功能置于分析中并不容易，因为它会被幻
想（fantasmatisée）。

This is why I come back to my capital Φ, which might just
as well be the first letter of the word fantasy. This letter situates
the relations of what I shall call a phunction of phonation. This
is the essence of the Φ , contrary to what people believe. It is a
phunction of phonation that finds itself being a placeh older for
the male, said to be man, as such In the Seminar Encore, I took a
stand against this Φ being a placeholder for the signifier that I



- 384 -

have only been able to sustain by means of a complicated letter
of mathematical notation, namely S(Ⱥ). S of barred A is
something altogether different from Φ. It is not that with which
man makes love. In the end, he makes love with h is
unconscious, and nothing more. As for what the woman
fantasizes, if this is really what the film presents us with, it is
something that, either way, impedes the encounter.

在这一点上，我要回到我的大写Φ（菲勒斯），它也可
以是幻想一词的首字母。这个字母定位了我将称之为语音
功能（phonction de phonation）的关系。与人们的想法相反，
是Φ 的本质，一个取代了男性的功能，即所谓的男人的语
音的功能，就是这样。在Encore 这期研讨班中，我反对了
Φ 作为一个我用复杂的数学符号来支撑的能指的容器，S(Ⱥ)
和Φ 是完全不同的东西。它不是那个男人用来做爱的东西。
归根结底，男人只用他的无意识做爱，而非任何其他东西。
女人的幻想，如果这就是那个电影告诉我们的，完全阻止
了相遇。

What is meant by S(Ⱥ)?
If the intermediary, that is to say, the instrument, that one
uses for copulation is really to be cast away, as is quite obvious,



- 385 -

this is not of the same order as what is at issue in my capital S of
barred capital A in brackets. The capital A is barred because
there is no Other - not where something makes up for this,
namely the Other as the locus of the unconscious, which I've just
said is what man makes love with, in another sense of the word
with, and that's what one's partner is -the big Other is barred
because there is no Other of the Other.

S(Ⱥ)是什么意思？
中介——或者说我们用来交媾的工具——显然被扔进
了垃圾桶。这与我的S(Ⱥ)中的危急情况不在同一个秩序中，
在S(Ⱥ) 中，大他者不存在，除了有补充（填补）
（suppléance）的地方，也就是说，大他者成为了无意识的
场域，那男人用来做爱的无意识，在另一种意义上，从
“用”这个字眼来说，无意识就是伴侣，大他者之所以被
划杠，是因为不存在大他者的大他者。

I beg your pardon for not having anything besides the bar
at my service. There is a bar that any woman knows how to get
her leg over, and that is the bar between signifier and signified,
as I hope the film I alluded to earlier will have proven for you. It
is like the bar that lies over x. There is another bar that consists



- 386 -

in barring. Moreover, I regret not having made it like the first
one, because it would have been most exemplary like that.
Positioned in diagonal through the capital A, this bar says that
there is no Other that could respond as a partner. The full
necessity of the human race is that there should be an Other of
the Other. This is what is generally called God, but which
analysis unveils as being quite simply La femme, The Woman.

我很遗憾，除了阻止我们达到目的的障碍，我们什么
也没找到。有那么一个障碍任何一个女人都知道如何越过，
那就是能指和所指之间的桥梁，就像我刚刚说的电影所展
示的。但还有另一个障碍，我将其放在Φ 之上（∀𝑋𝑋
തതതത ΦX ）。
我后悔没将它做的像他者一样，这样会更有示范意义。从
A 上斜划过去的杠言说着：不存在一个能作为伴侣而回应的
大他者——而人类的必然性在于有一个大他者的大他者。
他通常被称作上帝，但分析揭示了一个简单的结果，这个
上帝就是“女性”（La femme）。

The only thing that allows The Woman to be supposed is
that, like God, she should be a spawner. Only, the progress that
analysis leads us to make has been to have us realize that, even
though the myth makes her all issued from just one mother,



- 387 -

namely from Eve, there are only particular spawners. And this is
the sole thing that allows her to be denoted by means of the
definite article The, because I've told you that The Woman does
not exist, and I've voiced more and more reasons to believe it,
especially after seeing this film. It is in this respect that I called
to mind, in the Seminar Encore - so it would seem - what this
complicated letter means, namely the signifier of the fact that
there is no Other of the Other.

它被称作“女性”（La femme）的唯一原因是，既然我
告诉过你们““女性”不存在”（而且我有越来越多的原因
去相信这点，尤其是在我看完那电影之后），就像上帝，她
是“生殖的”。分析所要推进的仅仅是对于“La”的谜团的
重新认识，它将一切事物从同一个母亲中涌现出来，那个
母亲则是夏娃（Eve）；只有特殊（性）的母亲是生殖的。
“女性”（La femme）是不存在的，我相信这一点有源源不
断的原因，特别是在看过那部电影之后。关于这一点，我
想起了，在研讨班Encore 中，似乎那个复杂的字母的能指
之意义是：大他者的大他者不存在。

There you go. Everything I'm telling you here is nothing
but sense, sensible. In this capacity, it is heavy with risk, the risk



- 388 -

of going awry, as all history proves. We've never done anything
but that. If I'm taking these same risks, it is much rather to
prepare you for what else I might tell you, by trying to do some
folisophy, so to speak, that is less sinister than the book in the
Bible known as The Book of Wisdom, though in spite of
everything this is the best one can do to ground wisdom on lack,
which is the sole grounding it is able to have. It's really not bad
at all. It's top notch. I recommend that you read it. It is sober and
hits the right tone. Catholics seldom read it. One could even say
that over the centuries Catholicism has consisted in preventing
its followers from reading the Bible.

所以，我告诉你的一切都有其意义，因此充满了错误
的风险，所有的历史都证明了这一点。这就是我们所曾做
过的一切。如果我冒着同样的风险，那其实是为了让你为
我所要告诉你的其他东西做准备，我在试图产生一种比
《圣经》中的智慧之书更不邪恶的迷信（folisophy）。我其
实建议你们去读读《圣经》。它的语言是朴实的，很温和的。
天主教徒很少读它。甚至可以说，几个世纪以来，天主教
一直在阻止其信徒阅读圣经。但是为了在匮乏中发现智慧，
而这是发现智慧的唯一途径，它（圣经）其实一点也不差，
很了不起。



- 389 -


Will I manage to tell you - it oughtn't to be merely a dream
- what would qualify as an odd or end of the real, in the proper
sense of the word bout, which I specified earlier?

我是否能够告诉你——这一定不仅仅是一个梦——在
我不久前规定的部分（bout）的真正意义上，什么会被称为
部分的实在（bout de réel）？

For the time being, we may say that Freud himself
produced nothing but sensibleness, and this takes away all my
hope. This is not, however, a reason, not for me to hope to, but
for me really to do so someday. That's enough for today. One has
to laugh a bit once in a while.

就目前而言，可以说弗洛伊德本人只生产了有意义的
东西，而这使我失去了所有希望。就所有这些而言，这并
不是我应该仅仅希望做到的理由，而是我有一天要真正实
现的。今天就讲完了，偶尔人还是要笑一笑的。

1976 年3 月16 日




- 390 -

IX
FROM THE UNCONSCIOUS TO THE REAL
从无意识到实在
A new type of idea
Energetics and the real
Meaning is the Other of the real
The Name-of-the-Father：bypassing it, making use of it
The real is lawless

一种新类型的观念
能量论与实在
意义是实在界的大他者
父之名：绕过它，利用它
实在是无法无天的

I usually have something to tell you but today, because it's
a special occasion - it's my birthday - I should like to be able to
verify whether I know what I'm saying.

我通常有话要告诉你，但今天，因为这是一个特殊的
日子——今天是我的生日——我想验证一下我是否知道我



- 391 -

（自己）在说什么。

In spite of everything, the fact of saying aims to be heard. I
would like to verify whether, all in all, I don't content myself
with speaking for my own sake - as does everybody, of course,
if the unconscious carries a meaning.

尽管如此，说的目的是让别人听到。我想验证一下，
总的来说，我不满足于只为自己说话——当然，如果无意
识有一个意义的话，每个人都会这样做。

Today, therefore, I would prefer it if someone would ask
me a question. I said a question. I’m not asking for several. On
no account am I asking for miracles, for sparks to start flying.

因此，今天我更希望有人问我一个问题。我要的是
“一个”问题。我不要几个问题。我决不要求奇迹，也决
不要求问题如火花四溅。

What I would undoubtedly like most is for someone to
write something down that might justify the trouble I’ve been
going to for a little over twenty-two years now. The only way to



- 392 -

justify it would be for someone to invent something that might
serve me in some way. I’m of the mind that this is possible.

毫无疑问，我最希望的是将有人写下一些东西，也许
这东西可以证明我二十二年来所经历的麻烦。唯一能证明
这一点的方法就是有人发明了某个可能在某种程度上为我
服务的东西。我认为这是可能的。

1

I invented what has been written as the real.
Naturally, writing it down, real, does not suffice. A fair few
did that before I did. I have written this real in the form of the
Borromean knot, which isn't a knot but a link on account of
possessing certain properties. In the minimal form in which I
have traced out this link, there have to be at least three elements.
That's what the real is. The real consists in calling one of those
three real.

我发明了所谓的实在。
当然，把它写下来，实在（le Réel），这是不够的。在
我之前，有不少人这样做过。我以波罗米结的形式书写了



- 393 -

这个实在，它不是一个绳结，而是一个链结，因为它具有
某些属性。在我找到的这个最小形式的链结中，必须至少
有三个要素。这就是实在。实在在于称这三者中的一个为
实在。（The real consists in calling one of those three real）

This means that there are three elements, and these three, in
the way they are knotted together - well, actually, linked
together -form a metaphor. It's nothing more, of course, than a
metaphor of the link.

这意味着有三个要素，而这三个要素在它们联系在一
起的方式上——实际上，联系在一起——形成了一个隐喻。
当然，这只是一个链结的隐喻。

How can it be that there is such a thing as a metaphor of
something that is nothing but number? This metaphor, on
account of this, is called cipher.

怎么会有这样一个东西，一个只不过是数字的东西的
隐喻？因此，这个隐喻被称为密码（cipher）。

There are a certain number of ways of tracing out ciphers.



- 394 -

The simplest way is the one that I have called the unary trait.
Moreover, marking down a certain number of traits or points is
enough to indicate a number.

追踪密码的方法有很多种。最简单的方法是我称之为
一元特征的方法。此外，标记一定数量的特征或点就足以
表示一个数字。

What is known as energetics is none other than the
handling of a certain amount of numbers from which a constant
number is extracted. In referring to science such as it was
conceived of in his time, Freud took this as a reference point. He
just turned it into a metaphor. He never really gave any
grounding to the idea of psychical energetics. He wasn't even
able to draw metaphor from it with any vraisemblance, with any
plausibility. For example, the idea of a constant which would
bind stimulus to response can in no way be upheld.

所谓的能量论（energetics）就是处理一定数量的数字，
并从中提取出一个常数。在提到他那个时代所构想的科学
时，弗洛伊德以此为参照点。他只是把它变成了一个隐喻。
他从来没有真正给精神能量论的观念打下任何基础。他甚



- 395 -

至无法用任何逼真的方式、任何似是而非的方式从中得出
隐喻。例如，将刺激与反应结合在一起的恒常的观念是无
法被支持的。

I have been saying that I invented something in the
metaphor of the Borromean link. What does it mean, to invent?
And what have I invented? Is it an idea?

我一直在说我发明了一些关于波罗米结联系的隐喻。
去发明，意味着什么？我发明了什么？这是个观念吗？

Don't let this prevent you from trying in a short while to
pose me a question that will reward me - not for the effort that
I'm making right now, because, precisely, I think that for the
moment what I'm saying to you doesn't stand much of a chance
of drawing any response.

不要让这阻止你在短时间内向我提出一个问题来回报
我——不是因为我现在所做的努力，（而是）因为，确切地
说，我认为目前我对你说的话不太可能得到任何回应。

Is it an idea, this idea of the real such as it is written in the



- 396 -

Borromean knot, which, I insist, is actually a link? It's not an
idea, not one that can be upheld. This is where we touch upon
the fact
that the idea, the idea that occurs to us just like that when
we are lying down, is merely that, at least when reduced to its
analytic value - an idea that occurs to you when lying down.

这是一个观念吗？这种实在的观念，它被刻写在波罗
米结中，我坚持认为，它实际上是一种联系。这不是一个
可以坚持的观念。这就是我们接触到的观念的事实，我们
躺下时产生的观念，就像躺下时那样，仅仅是，至少当还
原为其分析价值时——躺下时你产生的观念。

Whether we're lying down or on our feet, the link effect
that we obtain through writing is not something that can be
pondered easily. In my experience, at the very least, a link
composed of a certain number of elements, even when they are
reduced to three, is something that can be neither easily
imagined nor easily written. One really has to make sure that
one is fairly well-versed in it if one is to be confident of
succeeding in providing its written form. It's precisely this that
you have witnessed umpteen times in my mistakes, well, in the



- 397 -

slips of my hand, when I stand before you trying to write
something that symbolizes this link.

无论我们是躺着还是站着，我们通过书写获得的链接
效果并不是一件容易思考的事情。根据我的经验，至少，
由一定数量的要素组成的链接，即使它们减少到三个，也
是不容易想象和书写的。如果一个人要想有信心成功地以
书面形式表达，就必须确保自己对此相当精通。正是因为
如此，当我站在你面前试图写一些象征这种联系的东西时，
好吧，即使在我的手中，你也已经见证了无数次我的错误。

I consider the fact of having stated the real in question in
the form of this writing to possess the value of what is generally
called a trauma. Not that my aim was to traumatize anyone,
especially not my audience, whom I have no reason whatsoever
to be mad at to the point of causing them a trauma. Let's say that
this is the forcing of a new kind of writing, which possesses,
through metaphor, a scope that really has to be called symbolic.
It is also the forcing of a new type of idea, if l may say so, an
idea that does not burgeon spontaneously from the simple doing
of that which forms meaning, that is to say, from the imaginary.




- 398 -

我认为，以这种书写的形式陈述了所讨论的“实在”，
这一事实具有一般所说的创伤的价值。并不是说我的目的
是要使某人创伤，尤其是我的观众，我没有任何理由对他
们发火，甚至给他们造成伤害。假设这是一种新的书写方
式的推动，通过隐喻，它拥有一个真正被称为“象征”的
范围。这也是一种新观念的形成，如果我可以这么说的话，
这种观念不是从简单的意义形成过程中自发产生的，也就
是说，从“想象”中产生的。

Nor is it the case that this should be something entirely
foreign. I would go further still by saying that this is what
renders palpable, that this is what lets us put our finger on, what
might be called reminiscence, albeit in an utterly illusory
fashion. Reminiscence consists in imagining, with respect to
something that holds the function of an idea but which is not one,
that one is reminiscing it, if I may express myself in this way.

这也不应该是完全陌生的东西。我想更进一步地说，
这是让我们触手可及的东西，这是我们可以用手指触摸的
东西，可以称之为回忆，尽管是以一种完全虚幻的方式。
回忆在于想象，对于具有某种思想功能但不是一种思想的
东西，如果我可以用这种方式表达自己的话，那就是一个



- 399 -

人在回忆它。

Reminiscence is distinct from remembering. These two
functions are set apart in Freud, who had a nose for distinctions.
Remembering
is
evidently
something
that
Freud
completely forced by virtue of the term impression. He
presumed that there were things in the nervous system that
became imprinted, and he furnished them with letters, which
was already to say too much because there is no reason for an
impression to be figured as this thing that we call a letter, which
is already considerably far removed from impression. A letter
and a phonological symbol are worlds apart.

回忆不同于记忆。弗洛伊德区分了这两种功能，他善
于区分。显然，记忆是弗洛伊德借助“印象”（impression）
一词完全强行定义的。他假设有一些东西会在神经系统中
留下印记，他给它们提供了一些字母，这已经说得太多了，
因为没有理由把一个印象描绘成我们称之为一个字母的东
西，这个东西已经离印象很远了。字母和语音符号是两个
截然不同的世界。

The idea that Freud attests to in the Project is to figure this



- 400 -

by means of networks, and this is perhaps what prompted me to
give them a new form, a more rigorous form, by turning them
into something that links up in a chain instead of simply
weaving together.

弗洛伊德在这个计划中证明的想法是通过网格来解决
这个问题，这也许正是促使我给它们一种新的形式，一种
更严格的形式，将它们变成一条链，而不是简单地编织在
一起。

Remembering amounts to introducing these links - and this
is certainly not an easy thing to do, the many slips I have made
in trying to trace out on this piece of paper the knots that were
placed under the patronage of the Borromeo family stand as
proof of this - into something that is already there and which
bears the name knowledge. Indeed, I tried to be rigorous by
saying that what Freud sustains as the unconscious always
presupposes a knowledge, and a spoken knowledge as such. The
unconscious is wholly reducible to a knowledge. This is the
minimum that is presupposed by the fact that the unconscious
may be interpreted.




- 401 -

记忆就等于把这些联系引入到已经存在的东西中——
这当然不是一件容易的事，我在试图在这张纸上找出波罗
米家族的族徽中（隐藏着）的结时犯了许多错误，这就是
证明——这些东西以知识为名。事实上，我试图严格地说，
弗洛伊德作为无意识所支持的东西总是以知识为前提，而
被言说的知识本身也是如此。无意识完全可以还原为知识。
这是无意识可以被解释这一事实所预设的最低限度。

It's quite clear that this knowledge has the minimum
requirement of two supports, which are called terms and which
are symbolized with letters, hence my way of writing knowledge
as supported by S subscript 2, S2. This is not S to the power of
two, it is not S squared, it is S supposed to be twain. The
definition I give of the signifier, which I support with S
subscript 1, S1, is to represent a subject as such, and to do so
truthfully. Truthfully on this occasion means in conformity with
reality.

很明显，这一知识有两个支撑的最小要求，这两个支
撑称为术语，用字母表示，因此我书写知识的方式是在S
下标2，S2。这不是S 对二的幂，不是S 的平方，而应该被
理解为twain（二）。我给能指下的定义，我在S 下标1，即



- 402 -

S1，指的是要如实地表征一个主体。老实说，在这种情况下
意味着符合现实。

The truthful is a fact of saying in conformity with reality.
On this occasion, reality is what functions, what functions
truthfully. However, what functions truthfully has nothing to do
with what I denote as real. That my real - for I really do have to
put it under my name - should condition reality, the reality of
your hearing, for example, is an altogether precarious
supposition. Here lies a gulf, and we are a long way from being
able to ensure that it is bridged.

“真实”（truthful）是一个与现实相符的事实。在这种
情况下，现实是什么在起作用，有什么在真实地起作用。
然而，真实的功能与我所说的“实在”无关。我的“实在”
——因为我真的必须把它放在我的名下——应该是现实的
条件，比如说，你听觉的现实，是一个完全不可靠的假设。
这里有一个分歧，我们在消除它之前还有很长的路要走。

In other words, the instance of knowledge that Freud
reiterates, I mean, renovates, in the shape of the unconscious,
does not on any account necessarily presuppose the real that I



- 403 -

make use of.

换言之，弗洛伊德重申的知识实例，我的意思是，革
新（renovates），以无意识的形式，在任何情况下都不一定
预设我所使用的实在。

I have been a vehicle for much of what is known as the
Freudian thing. I even gave the title The Freudian Thing to
something I wrote. When it comes to what I call the real,
however, I invented, because it imposed itself upon me. Perhaps
some of you here remember how and at what point this
notorious knot arose, this knot being all that is most figurative.
To say that to the imaginary and the symbolic, that is to say, to
things that are utterly foreign to one another, the real brings the
element that can make them hold together, is the maximum that
may be figured thereof.

我一直是弗洛伊德学说的载体（vehicle）。我甚至把我
写的东西命名为"弗洛伊德式的原物"（The Freudian Thing）。
然而，当谈到我所谓的“实在”时，我发明了它，因为它
被强加于我。也许在座的一些人还记得这个臭名昭著的结
是如何产生的，在什么时候产生的，这个结是最具象征性



- 404 -

的。对于想象和象征来说，也就是说，对于彼此完全陌生
的事物来说，“实在”带来了能够使它们结合在一起的要素，
这是其中可以描述的极限。

This is something which I can say I consider to be nothing
more than my symptom. I mean that it's my own particular way
of carrying Freud's lucubration to its degree of symbolism, to
the second degree - if indeed there is such a thing as may be
termed Freudian lucubration. Let's say that it's to the very extent
that Freud articulated the unconscious that I react to it.

我可以说，我认为这只是我的症状。我的意思是，这
是我自己独特的方式，将“弗洛伊德著作”（élucubration
freudienne）提升到其象征意义的程度，达到二阶的程度—
—如果真的有这样一种东西可以称为“弗洛伊德著作”的
话。让我们先假设弗洛伊德在某种程度上阐述了我对无意
识的回应吧。

Here we can already see that this is one way of carrying the
sinthome itself to the second degree. It is to the very extent that
Freud truly made a discovery - supposing this discovery to be
true - that it may be said that the real is my symptomatic



- 405 -

response.

在这里，我们已经可以看到，这是将圣状本身提升到
二阶的一种方式。弗洛伊德确实在一定程度上做出了一个
发现——假设这个发现是真的——可以说，实在是我的症
状式的回应。

To reduce this response to being symptomatic is also to
reduce any and every invention to the sinthome.

减少这种对症状的反应也就是减少任何与每项对圣状
的发明编造（invention）。

2

Let's change places.
Does one possess a memory? Might it be said that we do
more in saying that we have one than in imagining that we have
one, that we have one at our disposal? I should say that we have
it at our dire-sposal, that on a à dire.

让我们换个话题吧。



- 406 -

一个人有记忆吗？是否可以说，我们在说的过程中，
做了比想象我们有记忆更多的东西，我们有了这样一个任
我们处置的东西？我更愿意说"dire-sposal（言说-处置）"，
即它（指记忆）必须被言说[同样人们需要言说on a à dire]。

The tongue that I have called lalanglaise has all sorts of
resources for saying on a a dire. I have to tell. It's been
translated as J'ai a dire, which is an Anglicism. But the fact that
one may say not only have but also ought, I ought to tell,
produces a slide. J'ai a dire becomes Je dois dire. Likewise, in
the fact that one can accentuate the verb in such a way as to be
able to say I do make, I'm insisting, all told, on the fact that
through this making there is nothing but fashioning. There is
also the fact that one can equally separate out the negation in the
form I don't, which means I abstain from doing this or that. I
don't talk, which means I choose not to speak. To speak what? In
Joyce's case it was Gaelic.

我称之为“lalanglaise”（牙牙语）的语言有各种各样的
办法来表达（需要言说on a à dire）。我不得不（have）告诉
你。它已经被翻译成J'ai à dire（我有话要说），这是一种英
式说法。但人们可能会说，不仅不得不（have）而且是应该



- 407 -

（ought），我应该说，这一事实产生了一种幻觉。J'ai a dire
变成了Je dois dire（我应该说）不仅是“不得不”，而且还
有“应该”的意思——j'ai a dire 也可以译成“我应该说”。
同样，事实上，一个人可以用这样的方式（指将重音放在
动词上）强调这个动词，可以说“ 我确实做了（I do
make）”，我坚持认为，总而言之，通过这个做法，除了塑
造（fabrication）之外，什么都没有。还有一个事实，一个
人可以同样地以“我不”（I don't）的形式区分否定，这意
味着我不做这个或那个。我不说话（I don't talk），这意味着
我选择不说话（I choose not to speak）。说什么？在乔伊斯的
例子中是盖尔语。

This presupposes or implies that one chooses to speak the
tongue that one does effectively speak. In actual fact, one does
no more than imagine to oneself that one is choosing it.
Moreover, what resolves the issue is that, at the end of the day,
one creates this tongue. This is not restricted to the phases
during which a tongue is created. One creates a tongue
inasmuch as, from one instant to the next, one endues it with
meaning, one gives it a little nudge, without which it would not
be a living tongue. It is a living tongue to the extent that one
creates it from one instant to the next. It is in this respect that



- 408 -

there is no collective unconscious. There are only particular
unconsciouses, to the extent that every single one of us, from
one instant to the next, gives a little nudge to the tongue we
speak.

这预先假定或暗示了一个人选择说他实际上所说的语
言。事实上，人们只是想象自己正在选择它。此外，解决
这个问题的办法是，在一天结束时，一个人创造了这种语
言。这不限于创造语言的阶段。一个人创造了一种语言，
因为从一个瞬间到下一个瞬间，他赋予了它意义，他给了
它一点推动，没有这一点，它就不会是活的语言。它是一
种活生生的语言，从某种程度上说，人们从一个瞬间到下
一个瞬间都在创造它。正是在这方面，没有集体无意识。
只有特定的无意识，以至于我们每个人，从一个瞬间到下
一个瞬间，都会对我们所说的语言产生轻微的推动。

So, what is at issue for me is to find out whether I don't
know what I say to be true. It falls to each of you who are here
to tell me how you hear it. After all, it is by no means sure that
what I might say of the real amounts to anything more than
speaking out of place.




- 409 -

所以，对我来说，问题在于我是否不知道我说的是真
的。你们每个人都有责任告诉我你们是怎么听到它的。毕
竟，我所说的“实在”一点也不确定，它只不过是在说些
不恰当的话。

Saying that the real is a sinthome, my sinthome, does not
prevent energetics, which I spoke about earlier, from being any
less of one. What would the privilege of energetics boil down to?
Well, on the condition that things are handled in the right way,
in conformity with certain mathematical teachings, one always
finds a constant number. One does sense, however, that from
one moment to the next this is a requirement that is, as it were,
pre-set.

说“实在”是一个圣状，我的圣状，并不能阻止我前
面提到的能量论，使之成为一个不那么重要的学科。能量
论的特权可以归结为什么？好吧，在以正确的方式处理事
物的条件下，按照某些数学教导，人们总是能找到一个常
数。然而，人们确实感觉到，从一刻到下一刻，这是一个
预先设定（pre-set）的要求。

The thing that in and of itself constitutes energetics is that



- 410 -

some trick has to found in order to obtain the constant. The
suitable trick, the one that succeeds, is supposed to be congruent
with what is known as reality. But I draw a firm distinction
between, on the one hand, the supposed real, which is that organ,
so to speak, that has nothing whatsoever to do with an organ in
the flesh, by which imaginary and symbolic are tied together in
a knot, and, on the other hand, that which plays its part in
grounding the science of reality.

构成能量论的是，为了获得常数，必须找到一些技巧。
合适的技巧，即成功的技巧，应该与所谓的现实相符。但
我明确区分了，一方面，假想的“实在”，即器官，也就是
说，与肉体中的某一器官毫无关系的器官，通过它，“想象”
和“象征”结合在一起，另一方面，它在现实科学的基础
上发挥着作用。

The real at issue is illustrated by the fact that, in this planar
diagram of the knot, I show one field, the field of meaning, to be
essentially distinct from the real. One can say that the real both
carries and does not carry a meaning, in view of the fact that the
field of meaning is distinct from it.




- 411 -

“实在”的争论在这一事实得到了说明，在这个（波
罗米）结的平面图中，我展示了一个场域，意义的场域
（field of meaning），其本质上与实在界不同。可以说，鉴
于意义的场域与之不同，“实在”既有意义也没有意义。

That the real carries no meaning is what is figured by
meaning being over there and the real being over here.

“实在”没有意义，这是由“存在于那里”和“实在
存在于这里”的意义所决定的。


The true hole lies here

真实的空洞位于此处




- 412 -

The symbolic is distinct in being specified, as it were, as a
hole. What is striking, however, is that the true hole is here, at
the site where it is revealed that there is no Other of the Other.

“象征”的不同之处在于它被指定为一个孔洞。然而，
令人震惊的是，真实的空洞位于此处，就在这个地方，它
被揭示出没有大他者的大他者。

This would be its place, just as meaning is the Other of the
real, but there is nothing of the like. In the place of the Other of
the Other, there is no order of existence. It is precisely in this
respect that I am given to think that the real has no existence
either.

这将是它的位置，就像意义是实在界的大他者一样，
但没有其他类似的东西。在大他者的大他者的位置，没有
实存的秩序（order of existence）。正是在这方面，我认为实
在没有实存

I am given to think that the real lies in abeyance, as it were.
It might be what I've reduced it to, in the form of a question,
namely, nothing but a response to Freud's lucubration, which,



- 413 -

anyway, one may say is quite repellent to energetics. It is utterly
up in the air as far as this energetics is concerned.

我认为，实在的问题在于搁置（abeyance）。这可能是
我把它简化为的一个问题的形式，也就是说，只是对弗洛
伊德著作的回应，不管怎样，有人可能会说，这样做是非
常排斥能量论的。就这个能量论而言，它完全是空中楼阁。

The sole conception that is able to make up for the said
energetics is the one that I have set out using the term real.

唯一能够弥补上述能量论的概念正是我所用的“实在”。

3

[The written questions are read out by Lacan]

[下述问题是由拉康宣读的]

I'm being asked the fallowing - If psychoanalysis is a
sinthome - I didn't say that psychoanalysis was a sinthome - is it
not the case that what you've been doing with your knot and



- 414 -

your mathemes is to decipher psychoanalysis, with the
consequence of dissipating its signification?

我被打趣地提问到：
Q1：精神分析是否一个圣状？（拉康：我没有说过精
神分析是一个圣状）你对你的结和数学型所做的不就是为
了破译精神分析，其结果正是消解它的意义吗？

I don't think psychoanalysis is a sinthome. I think it's a
practice whose effectiveness, which despite everything is
palpable, implies for me that I should make what I have called
my knot, namely the triple knot up there on the blackboard. It is
in this respect that I have been holding in abeyance the approach
to the third element that is distinct from reality and which I call
the real. It is in this respect too that I cannot say I think, because
this is a thinking that is still utterly sealed off, that is to say,
ultimately enigmatic.

A：我不认为精神分析是一个圣状。我认为这是一种有
效的练习，尽管一切都是显而易见的，但对我来说，这意
味着我应该制做我所称的“我的结”，即黑板上的三重结。
正是在这方面，我一直搁置对第三要素的探索，其不同于



- 415 -

现实，（而正是）我称之为的实在。也正是在这方面，我不
能说“我在思考”（I think），因为这是一种仍然完全封闭的
思想，也就是说，最终是令人费解的。

The real's distinction from reality is something that I am
not sure merges with what I shall call the proper value that I
ascribe to the term real. Since the real is devoid of meaning, I'm
not sure whether the meaning of this real might not be clarified
when it is held to be nothing less than a sinthome.

“实在”（Real）与“真实”（reality）的区别，我不确
定是否与我所说的“实在”一词的正确价值相融合。因为
实在是没有意义的，我不确定当它被认为是一个圣状时，
这个实在的意义是否会被澄清。

This is how I'm replying to the question that has been put to
me.
这就是我对这个问题的回答。

I believe that I am able to support what is at issue by means
of a rough-and-ready topology, and what is at issue is the very
function of the real, which I set apart from what I believe I am



- 416 -

able to hold with certainty to be the unconscious. With certainty,
because I am practised - am I not? - in the latter. It is to the
extent that the unconscious is not without reference to the body
that I think that the function of the real can be set apart from it.

我相信我能够通过一个粗线条的的拓扑结构来支持争
论的东西，争论的是实在的功能，我把实在与我相信我可
以肯定地持有的无意识区分开来。毫无疑问，因为就后者
而言我是有经验的。正是在某种程度上，无意识并非没有
提及身体，我认为实在的功能可以从中分离出来。

I do think that the psychoanalyst cannot be conceived of
otherwise than as a sinthome. Psychoanalysis is not the
sinthome, the psychoanalyst is. This is how I reply to what's
been put to me by way of a question.

我确实认为精神分析师只能被认为是一个圣状。精神
分析不是圣状，精神分析师才是。这就是我对这个问题的
回答。

- According to Genesis, as translated by Andre Chouraqui -
I'm reading you the things that people have been good enough to



- 417 -

write down for me, which is no worse than anything else, given
what I said about the real abiding by writing - God created for
man une aide contre lui, a helpmeet against him. What about the
psychoanalyst as a helpmeet against?

Q2：根据安德烈·舒拉基翻译的《创世纪》——我在
给你读人们足够好以为我写下的东西，没有其他比这更糟
的东西了，考虑到我所说的关于实在的内容，它被以文字
形式记录下来——上帝创造了人类，一个反对他的帮手。
精神分析师也是一个反对的帮手吗？（aide contre）

The psychoanalyst is a helpmeet that may be said to be a
reversal in the terms of Genesis, since the Other of the Other too
is what I defined just a moment ago as a little hole. That this
little hole should be able all by itself to form a helpmeet is
precisely where the hypothesis of the unconscious finds its
support.

A：精神分析师是一个助手（une aide），这可以说成是
于《创世纪》中的一个逆转，大他者的大他者也是我刚才
定义的一个小洞。这个小洞本身应该能够使一个助手出现，
这正是无意识预设得到支持的地方。



- 418 -


The hypothesis of the unconscious, and Freud underscores
this, is something that can only hold up by presupposing the
Name-of-the-Father. Presupposing the Name-of-the-Father,
which is certainly God, is how psychoanalysis, when it succeeds,
proves that the Name-of-the-Father can just as well be bypassed.
One can just as well bypass it, on the condition that one make
use of it.

弗洛伊德强调的无意识预设只能通过预设父之名来支
持。预设的父之名，当然是上帝，是精神分析学在成功证
明父之名同样可以被绕过。只要你能利用它，你自然也可
以绕过它。

Q- Isn't each speech act, the exploit of one particular
unconscious, a collectivization of the unconscious?

Q3：不是每一个言说行为，一个特定无意识的利用，
都是无意识的集体化吗？

If each speech act is the exploit of one particular
unconscious then it is absolutely clear, as the theory has shown,



- 419 -

that each speech act may stand a hope of being a fact of saying.
Moreover, this fact of saying ends up in what there is a theory
for, the theory that supports every kind of revolution, namely the
theory of contradiction.

A：如果每一个言说行为都是某一特定无意识的利用，
那么正如该理论所表明的那样，每一个话语行为都有希望
成为一个事实。此外，这种说法的事实最终导致了一种理
论，一种支持各种革命的理论，即矛盾理论。

One can say things of very great diversity, each thing being
on any given occasion a contradiction. But that a reality that is
presumed to be revolutionary should emerge from this is very
precisely what has never been proven. I mean that just because
there might be some contradictory hullabaloo, it doesn't mean
that anything like a constituent of reality should ever have arisen
from it. One hopes that a reality shall arise from it, but this is
just what has never materialized as such.

人们可以说各种各样的事情，每件事在任何特定场合
都是矛盾的。但是，一个被认为是革命性的现实应该由此
产生，这正是从未被证明的事实。我的意思是，仅仅因为



- 420 -

可能会有一些相互矛盾的喧嚣，并不意味着任何类似于现
实组成部分的东西都会由此产生。人们希望从中产生一个
现实，但这正是从未实现的。

- What limits do you set to the fields of metaphor?
Q4：你对隐喻的领域设置了什么限制？

A：That's a very good question. Just because the straight
line is infinite, it doesn't mean that it's limitless. Indeed, the
question continues -Are the fields of metaphor infinite, like the
straight line for instance?

A：这是一个很好的问题。仅仅因为直线是无穷尽的，
这并不意味着它是无限的。事实上，问题还在继续——隐
喻的领域是无限的吗，比如直线？

It's quite certain that the status of the straight line deserves
to be reflected on. The fact that a straight line is assuredly finite
when it is cut, on account of having limits, does not mean that
an infinite straight line is limitless. Just because a finite line has
limits, it doesn't mean that an infinite straight line suffices when
it comes to metaphorizing the infinite, since an infinite straight



- 421 -

line may be supposed to have what is known as a point at
infinity, that is, it may be supposed to form a circle.

可以肯定的是，直线的状态值得反思。事实上，由于
有限制，直线在被切割时确实是有限的，但这并不意味着
一根无限长的直线是无限的。仅仅因为有限长的线有其极
限，这并不意味着无限长的直线就足以隐喻无限，因为无
限长的直线可能被认为在无限远处有一个点，也就是说，
它可能被认为形成一个圆环。

The question that the straight line poses is precisely the
following -that the straight line is not straight. The ray of light
might seem to afford us an image of the straight line, but
everyone knows, as seems to be the case going by the latest
news from Einstein, it has to be reckoned to be flexible. The
light ray bends, even though it does give every appearance of
realizing the straight line of this limited scope of ours.

直线所指出的问题正是如此——直线不直。光线似乎
为我们提供了直线的图像，但每个人都知道，正如爱因斯
坦的最新消息所显示的那样，它必须被认为是灵活的。光
线会弯曲，尽管它确实让我们在有限的范围内看到了直线。



- 422 -


How are we to form some conception of a straight line that,
on certain occasions, bends? This is evidently a problem that is
raised by my question of the real. This question im-plies in some
way that one can ask questions, my goodness, like the one Lenin
asked. It is expressly said that a straight line may be curved. He
im-ply-cated this in his metaphor that supposes that a stick-a
stick being, roughly speaking, the image of a straight line -
simply by virtue of being a stick, can be bent and, by the same
token, is capable of being straightened.

我们如何在某些情况下形成直线弯曲的概念？这显然
是我的实在问题所引出的一个问题。这个问题意味着在某
种程度上一个人可以问问题，我的天哪，就像列宁问的那
样。明确地说，直线可以是弯曲的。他在他的比喻中解释
了这一点，假设一根棍子——粗略地说，一个直线的图象
——仅仅因为是棍子，它可以被弯曲，同样，也可以被拉
直。



- 423 -


What is the meaning of this straightening in relation to the
use that we can make of it in the Borromean knot with two
straight lines? What might the definition of the straight line be
beyond the support of what is called - with limited scope - the
light ray? There is no other support for it besides what is known
as the shortest path from one point to another. But how is one to
know what the shortest path from one point to another is?

与我们在具有两条直线的波罗米结中的使用相关，这
种拉直（或矫直）的含义是什么？直线的定义可能超出了
所谓的（范围有限）光线的支持范围吗？除了从一点到另
一点的最短路径之外，没有其他支持。但是，人们如何知
道从一个点到另一个点的最短路径是什么呢？

- I keep expecting you to play on the equivoques. You have
said Y a d'l'Un, you've spoken to us about the real as impossible,



- 424 -

but you haven't drafted in Un-possible. In relation to Joyce you
have spoken about imposed words, but you haven't drafted in
the Name-of-the-Father as Un-posé.

Q5：我一直在期待你在模棱两可的话上玩游戏。你说
过“有这么一个‘一’”（Y a d'l'Un），你跟我们谈过“不可
能的实在”，但你没有把“Un-possible”（一种可能）写进去。
关于乔伊斯，你说了一些强词夺理的话，但你并没有把Un-
posé 写入父之名中。

This one is signed. Who is it that keeps expecting me to
play on blessed equivoques? I'm not especially keen on doing so.
It seems to me that I demystify them. It's quite certain that I'm in
a considerable bind with this Un, this One. I don't know what to
do with it, because the One is not a number, as everybody knows,
and as I have stressed on occasion.

A：这张是署名了的。是谁一直期待我在玩这种模棱两
可的语言游戏（愠怒）？我并不特别想这样做。在我看来，
我是在揭开他们的神秘面纱。很肯定我和这个Un，（太）
一，有很大的关系。我不知道该怎么处理它，因为这个
“一”不是一个数字，每个人都知道，正如我偶尔强调的



- 425 -

那样。

I speak of the real as impossible to the extent that I believe,
precisely, that the real - well, I believe, if this is my symptom,
then pray tell me so - it really has to be said, the real is lawless.
The true real implies the absence of any law. The real has no
order. This is what I mean when I say that the only thing that I
might one day manage to spell out here in front of you is
something that has to do with what I called an odd or end of the
real.

我说实在是不可能的，因为我确实相信实在——好吧，
“我相信”，如果这是我的症状，那么请告诉我——必须说，
实在是非法的。真正的实在意味着没有任何法律（律令）。
实在没有秩序。这就是我的意思，当我说有一天我能在你
们面前说出来的唯一一件事，就是我所谓的“odd（古怪）”
或“实在的终结”。

- What do you make of the contradictory hullabaloo that's
been going on for a few years now in China?

Q6：你对中国这几年来一直存在的自相矛盾的喧嚣有



- 426 -

何看法？

I've been waiting, but I don't hope for anything.

A：我一直在等，但我什么都不期望。

- A point is defined as the intersection of three planes. Can
one say that it is real? In setting down traits in writing, as an
alignment of points, are these points real, in the sense that you
understand it?

Q7：一个点被定义为三个平面的交点。可以说这是实
在吗？这些点在书写中设定特征，作为点的排列，在你听
到的意义上是实在的吗？

It is written here au sens que vous l'entendez. No, there's
nothing to laugh at. This is a question that absolutely deserves to
be asked. A point is defined as the intersection of three planes.
Can one say that it's real?

A：这是书写在这里的“在你听到的意义上”（in the



- 427 -

sense that you understand it, au sens que vous l’entendez）58。
不，这没什么好笑的。这是一个绝对值得提出的问题。一
个点定义为三个平面的交点。能说这是实在吗？

The im-ply-cation of what I have been calling the
Borromean link is that amid everything that is consistent in this
link there is no common point. This im-ply-cation certainly
excludes the point as such from the real. That a figuration of the
real should be able to be supported only by the hypothesis that
there is no common point, no branching, no Y in writing,
certainly implies that the real does not entail the point as such. I
am very grateful for this question.

我一直称之为波罗米链结的意义在于，在这个链结中
所有一致的东西中，没有共同点。这一链结的含义无疑将
这一点排除在实在之外。实在的形象应该只能由假设来支
持，即在文字中没有共同点，没有分支，没有书写中的Y，
这无疑意味着实在并不包含这样的点。我非常感谢这个问
题。

- Does the constant number that you've been speaking

58 Entendre 在法语里面有听到和理解的意思。



- 428 -

about bear any relation to the phallus or the phallic function?

Q8：你所说的常数与菲勒斯或菲勒斯函数有关系吗？

I think that - well, I think, I do think nevertheless that my
thought is more than a symptom - that the phallus absolutely
cannot be a sufficient support for what Freud conceived of as
energetics. Furthermore, what is altogether striking is that Freud
himself never identified it.

A：我认为-嗯，我认为，我确实认为我的想法不仅仅
是一种症状——菲勒斯绝对不能充分支持弗洛伊德所认为
的能量论。此外，令人震惊的是，弗洛伊德本人从未识别
出这一点。

Someone's written to me in Chinese, which is really very
nice. No in Japanese, I mean that I recognize the small
characters. I should very much like the person who sent me this
text to translate it for me.

有人用中文给我写信，真是太好了。不对，是用日语
写的，我的意思是我能认出细微的特征。我应该非常喜欢



- 429 -

给我发这篇文章的人来帮我翻译。

- Are you an anarchist?

Q9：你是无政府主义者吗？

Certainly not.

A：当然不是。

- What might the status be of a response given to a
lucubration on the basis of which it would be defined as a
sinthome?

Q10：在一种被定义为圣状的基础上的回应——对一种
深入研究的回应（lucubration）——究竟是处在什么位置上？

Earlier I mentioned a lucubration which is the lucubration
of the unconscious. You surely noticed that I had to bring the
sinthome down a peg in order to consider it to be homogenous
with the lucubration of the unconscious. I mean that the
sinthome was figured as being tied to the unconscious in a knot.



- 430 -

What I supposed earlier was that I was reducing the sinthome,
which is here, to something that corresponds not to the
lucubration of the unconscious but to the reality of the
unconscious. It's quite certain that, even in this form, this
implies a third term, one that keeps these two rings of string
apart.

A：早些时候我提到了一种深入研究，即对无意识的深
入研究。你一定注意到了，我不得不把症状的情况弄得一
团糟，这样才能认为它与无意识的深入研究是同质的。我
的意思是，症状被认为是和无意识绑在同一个结上的。我
之前所想的是，我把圣状简化了，它就在这里，与对无意
识的深入研究无关，而是与无意识的现实有关。可以肯定
的是，即使在这种形式下，这也意味着有一个第三项，使
这两个绳环分开。




- 431 -


The link between the sinthome and the unconscious maintained
by the body

介于症状与无意识之间的链结，被身体维持着

This third term can be whatever you like, but if the
sinthome is considered to be equivalent to the real, on this
occasion the third term can only be the imaginary. After all, one
can repeat Freud's theory by making this imaginary, to wit, the
body, everything that holds the two apart - the two of the set that
I have formed here by means of a knot between the symptom
and the symbolic.

这第三项可以是你喜欢的任何东西，但如果圣状被认



- 432 -

为等同于实在的话，在这种情况下，第三项只能是想象。
毕竟，我们可以重复弗洛伊德的理论，通过想象，也就是
说，身体，所有将两者分开的东西-我在这里通过症状和象
征之间的链结形成的集合中的两个。

I thank you for having sent me your questions, apart from
this one

我感谢你把你的问题发给我，除了这个问题

- Is your twisted cigar a symptom of your real?

Q11：你那扭曲的雪茄是你实在的症状吗？

It certainly is. My twisted cigar bears the strictest possible
relation to the question that I posed about the equally twisted
straight line of the same name.

A：那当然。我的扭曲雪茄，与我提出的有着相同名称
的，那同样扭曲的直线的问题，有着最严格的联系。

1976 年4 月13 日



- 433 -

X
THE WRITING OF THE EGO
自我的书写
What philosophy lacks, and what makes up for it
On a logic of bags and cords
Disentangling oneself from the idea of eternity
On a body dropped like a fruit peel
What Joyce's knot lacks, and what rectifies it

什么是哲学所缺乏的，和什么填补了这个缺乏
一种袋子与绳的逻辑
从永恒的观念的缠绕中解开自己
一具像果皮一样脱去的身体
乔伊斯的结缺乏了什么，以及什么修补它

Last time, I confided in you that the strike suited me just
fine. I hadn't the faintest inclination to tell you anything
whatsoever, because I was in a pickle. Can you hear me? I'm not
going to speak very loudly. Is this microphone on? It would be
quite easy for me to find some other pretext, like this
microphone not working, for instance - not that this time I don't
have something to tell you.



- 434 -

Last time, I was too tangled up in my knots and Joyce to
have the slightest inclination to speak to you about it. I was in a
pickle, but now I am a little less so, because I think I've found a
few transmissible items.
Evidently I've been rather active. Difficulty spurs me on,
such that I've been toiling away throughout all my weekends
racking my brains over something that doesn't go without saying
- since my having found the so-called Borromean knot does not
go without . saying.

上次，我向你们承认罢课对我很合适；因为我不想告
诉你任何事，这使我自己很不安。找些别的借口对我来说
是很容易的——比如说麦克风坏了——而不是这次我没有
什么话要对你们说。但事实上，上次我太过纠结于结和乔
伊斯，而根本不想和你们说话。我感到不安。现在我稍微
不那么认为了，因为我认为我发现了一些东西，最终发现
了一些可传播的东西。我一直很活跃，很明显——我的意
思是，这个难题激起了我，所以我每个周末都在拼命地想
办法解决一些挥之不去的问题。最终，我找到了所谓的波
罗米结，这是我试图强加的一些事情，因为乔伊斯对波罗
米结完全没有概念。这并不是说他没有利用圆圈和十字。
事实上，他们什么也没说，事实上，有一位克莱夫·哈特的，



- 435 -

致力于评点乔伊斯的著名学者，在他题为《詹姆斯·乔伊斯
的结构》的书中大量使用了圆圈和十字，尤其是关于《芬
尼根的守灵夜》。

1

The first thing I can tell you, then, is this: I wish that the
expression 'It must be done', in current usage, had not been so
over used, as it fits so naturally the fabrication of the knot: it
must be done. What does that mean? It comes down to writing it.
What is striking, curious, Is that this knot which I describe as
Borromean - you should know why - is a support for thinking. I
will allow myself to illustrate this with a term which I must
write: 'support for un-thinking' [appui à l’appense], allowing la
pens,e to be written differently. It is a support for thinking which
justifies the writing I have just put on that little piece of white
paper for you, it is a support for thinking, for 'un-thinking'. But
it is curious that this 'support' is necessary, that it has to be
written to get anything from it, because it is quite apparent that
it is not easy to represent this chain - for it is a chain, not a knot,
in reality - it is not easy to see how this Borromean chain only
functions in thinking (how cutting la from penser). It is not easy,



- 436 -

even at the simplest level, and this indicates what the knot
brings with it. It must be written to see how it functions, this
noeud Borromean. Which makes one think of something
mentioned somewhere in Joyce, ‘on Mount Nebo the law was
given to us’.

那么，我可以告诉你的第一件事就是：我希望“它必须
被完成”这个表达，在当前的用法中没有被过度使用，因为
它很自然地符合结的构成：它必须被完成（faut le faire）。
这是什么意思呢？归根结底就是写作。令人惊讶和好奇的
是，我把这个结描述为波罗米结——你们应该知道为什么
——是对思考的帮助。我将允许自己用一个我必须写的术
语来说明这一点：“思想的支柱”[appui à l’appensée]，la
pensée（思想）允许以不同的方式被书写。这是一种对思考
的支持，它证明了我刚才为你们所写在白纸上的东西，它
是思考的支柱，是“非思考”（un-thinking）的支柱。但不寻
常的是，这种“支柱”是必要的，它必须被写下来才能从中获
取某些东西，因为很明显，要表现这条链并不容易——因
为事实上它是一条链，而不是一个结——并不容易发现这
条波罗米链如何仅在思考中发挥作用（如何从penser［思考］
中切下la）。即使是在最简单的层面上，这也并不容易，并
且这表明了结带来了什么。这个波罗米结，它必须被写下，



- 437 -

以观察它是如何运作的。不禁让人想起乔伊斯在某处提到
的一句话，“在尼波山上，律法是赐给我们的”。

A writing is thus an act [un faire] which provides a support
for thinking. Properly speaking, the noeud bo in question
completely changes the meaning of writing. It gives writing an
autonomy, and an autonomy which is all the more remarkable in
that there is another writing, which is what Derrida has
emphasised, namely the result of what could be termed a
precipitation of the signifier. Derrida has laid emphasis on this,
but it is quite clear that 1 showed him the way, for the fact that I
found no other means of supporting the signifier than writing it
'S' is already a sufficient indication. But what remains is that the
signifier, in other words what is modulated in the voice, has
nothing to do with writing. This, in any case, is what is perfectly
demonstrated by my noeud bo. It changes the meaning of
writing, it shows that there is something to which signifiers can
be attached - and how can these signifiers be attached? Through
the intermediaiy of what I term dit-mension - in my way of
writing It: the mention of speech. The advantage of that way of
writing it is that 'mention' can be extended to mensonge ['lie'],
thus indicating that what is said is not necessarily true.



- 438 -


因此，写作是一种为思考提供支柱的行为[un faire]。严
格的说，所讨论的波罗米结完全改变了写作的意义。它赋
予了写作一种自主性，一种更为显著的自主性，因为还有
另一种写作，这是德里达所强调的，即可以称之为能指沉
淀（a precipitation of the signifier）的结果。德里达强调了这
一点，但很明显，是我为他指明了道路，因为事实上，我
发现除了书写它为“S”之外，没有其他支持能指的方式了，
这已经是一个充分的指示。但剩下的是能指，换句话说，
声音中被调节的东西，与写作无关。无论如何，这正是我
的波罗米结所完美证明的。它改变了写作的意义，它表明
有一些能指可以依附的东西——这些能指如何依附呢？通
过我称之为言说的维度（dit-mension）的中介——以我的写
作方式：言说的提及( the mention of speech)。这种写作方式
的优点是“提及”可以扩展到谎言 ['lie']，从而表明所说的不
一定是真的。

In other words, the speech [dit] which results from what is
called philosophy is not without a certain lack, a lack which I
am trying - trying - to fill [supplêer] with recourse to what can
only be written in the noeud Borromean, what can only be
written for something to be taken from it. It is no less true that



- 439 -

what there is of [philia/φιλία] in philosophy can take on a
significance: that of time as it is thought. And what I put
forward is that writing in this instance changes the meaning, the
mode of what is at stake - and what is at stake is this
[philia/φιλία] of wisdom. What is wisdom? It is what is not very
easy to support other than with the writing of the nœud
Borromean Itself So that in the end - forgive my infatuation -
what I am doing, what I am trying to do with my knot, is
nothing less than the first philosophy which, it seems to me, can
be supported. This is simply the introduction of these noeud
Borromean, of the idea that they support a bone [os], a bone
which is as it were a sufficient indication of something I'll term
os-bjet, which is certainly what characterises the letter which I
link to it, the letter a. And if I reduce this os-bjet to a, it Is
precisely in order to mark the fact that the letter here is nothing
less than a testimony to the intrusion of a writing as other -as
'other' with, precisely, a small a. The writing in question comes
from somewhere other than the signifier.

换言之，由所谓哲学产生的话语［dit（说）］并非没有
某种缺失，某种我正试图用只能写在波罗米结中的东西来
填补[supplêer]的缺失，只能写出来以获取某种东西，某种



- 440 -

只有写下来才能从中取走的东西。同样正确的是，哲学中
philia [φιλία]（友爱）可以承担一个意义：关于时间的看法
（that of time as it is thought）。我提出的是，在这种情况下，
写作改变了意义，改变了处于危险中的模式——而处于危
险之中的是智慧的philia [φιλία]。智慧是什么？除了波罗米
结本身的写作之外，这不是很容易支撑的东西，因此最终
——原谅我的痴迷——我正在做的，我试图用我的结做的，
在我看来并不亚于第一哲学，它可以被支撑。这仅仅是介
绍这些波罗米结的概念，它们支撑了一个骨骼［os（骨）］，
它是一个充足的对某物的指示，我将其称为os-bjet59，这当
然是我与它链接的字母，字母a 的特征（os-bjet a）。如果我
将这个os-bjet 简化为a，正是为了表明这样一个事实，即这
里的字母（letter）无异于证明一种书写（writing）作为他者
——作为“他者”的侵入，确切地说，是一个小a。有问题的
书写（writing）来自能指以外的其他地方。

My interest in this business of writing does not date from
yesterday - the first time I put it forward was when I spoke
about the unitary trait, the elnziger Zug in Freud. I have given
this unitary trait another support through the Borromean knot,
which I haven't yet shown you - in my notes I have written it DI

59 精神是块骨头，这块骨头正是客体小a，就是拉康基本幻想公式。译注



- 441 -

(droite infinie, 'straight line'). The straight line in question - it is
not the first time you've heard me talk of it - is something I
characterize as equivalent to the circle. By combining two
straight lines and a circle, you have the essentials of the
Borromean knot. Why does the straight line have this quality?
Because it is the best Illustration of the hole. Topology shows us
that there is a hole in the middle of a circle, and one even starts
trying to imagine what its centre would be, which extends to all
sorts of expressions (the nervous centre, for instance, which no-
one knows the exact meaning of). The straight line has the
quality of having the hole all around it. It is the simplest support
of the hole.

我对写作的兴趣并不是从昨天开始的——我第一次提
出它是在我谈到单一特征（unitary trait）时，弗洛伊德所说
的“一个个别的特征”（elnziger-Zug）。我通过波罗米结给了
这个单一特征另一个支撑，我还没有向你们展示——在我
的笔记中，我把它写作DI（droite infinie，“无限的直线”）。
问题中的直线——这不是你们第一次听到我谈论它——是
某些我描述为与圆（circle）相等的东西。（图1）通过将两
条直线和一个圆结合起来，你就拥有了波罗米结的本质
（essentials）。（图2）为什么直线具有这种性质？因为这是



- 442 -

这个洞最好的说明。拓扑学告诉我们，在圆的中间有一个
洞，人们甚至开始想象它的中心是什么，这延伸到各种各
样的表达（例如，焦虑的中心，没有人知道它的确切含义）。
直线的特征是周围有洞环绕，它是洞最朴素的支持。

So what does this give us as a reference to analytic practice?
That man, and not God, is made up of a trinity of what we'll call
elements. What is an element? An element is that which makes
One, in other words the unitary trait; that which makes One, and
which, because of this, sets in motion substitution. The
characteristic of an element is that it goes into a combinatory.
Thus, Real,Imaginary and Symbolic - which after all amounts, it
seems to me, to the other triad which, according to Aristotle,
goes to make up man: νουσ，ψυχη，σωμα (or will, intelligence
and affectivity).

那么，这给了我们什么作为分析实践的参考呢？那个
人，而不是上帝，是由我们称之为要素（element）的三位
一体构成的。什么是一个要素？一个要素是构成“一”的要素，



- 443 -

换句话说，单一特征（unitary trait）；它创造了“一”，并且
因此，它使取代（作用）（substitution）运转。要素的特征
是它会进入一个组合（combinatory）。因此，实在、想象和
象征——在我看来，这毕竟等同于另一个三位一体，根据
亚里士多德，这三元组构成了人： νοῦς（努斯），ψυχή（灵
魂），σῶμα（身体）（或意志，智力和情感）。

What I am trying to introduce with this writing is nothing
less than what I'll call a logic of the sack and the cord; because
there is obviously a sack whose myth, as it were, consists in the
sphere. But nobody, it seems, has sufficiently considered the
consequences of the introduction of the cord, that the cord
proves that a sack is not closed unless it is tied, and that in every
sphere we must imagine something, which is of course at every
point of the sphere and which knots it, making it something one
can blow into, knots it with a cord.

我试图通过这篇文章来介绍的，正是我所说的袋子和
绳的逻辑；因为很明显，有一个袋子，它的神话，可以说
